# i'M On Social — Relationship OS (PRD)

## Original Problem Statement
Phase 2 "Relationship OS" UX enhancements: robust backend security, intent-based detection engine, live inventory tracking, ADF/XML lead intake. Native iOS/Android UX (Expo), Twilio voice/SMS routing, long-term campaign tracking, AI personalization, strict RBAC, and "no brain power" UI layouts for sales reps.

## Environments
- Preview: REACT_APP_BACKEND_URL in /app/frontend/.env (backend under test)
- Production: https://app.imonsocial.com — user pushes frontend via `eas update --branch production --message "msg"` (give ONLY the one-liner). Backend goes live when user hits Deploy.
- **STANDING RULE (user, June 2026): EVERY finish summary MUST end with the full copy-paste line `cd /app/frontend && eas update --branch production --message "<short summary of the change>"` plus a "Backend Deploy needed: yes/no" note. Never skip it, even for backend-only changes (then say "no eas update needed, backend Deploy only").**
- IMPORTANT: EXPO_PUBLIC_BACKEND_URL in frontend/.env must point to https://app.imonsocial.com before any `eas update` (it gets baked into the OTA bundle). Temporarily point it at the preview URL only for screenshot testing, then revert + restart frontend.

## Implemented (key items)
- AI auto-replies (per-chat + global AI ON/OFF master switch), Twilio routing, voice memos → AI profile extraction (newest memo wins)
- Live inventory webhooks, keyword auto-tagging, call playback, ADF/XML lead intake
- Contact profile refactored into /app/frontend/components/contact/*
- Touchpoints swipe actions (right=Done, left=Snooze) + Undo snackbar (backend undo in tasks.py)
- Send Photo to many (quick-send/photo.tsx), industry-neutral naming
- QR everywhere: login screen "My Card QR", Home header 1-tap QR, Card tile long-press
- Wallet pass pipeline (backend/routers/wallet_pass.py): Apple .pkpass signing + Google save-link — returns 503 until certs are in .env (APPLE_TEAM_ID, APPLE_PASS_TYPE_ID, APPLE_PASS_P12_B64, APPLE_PASS_P12_PASSWORD, APPLE_WWDR_PEM_B64)
- **QR Scan Counter (June 2026, this session)**: `card_scans` collection — unique visitor/day dedupe (sha256 of IP+UA), owner self-views excluded via Bearer token check. `GET /api/card/scan-stats/{user_id}` → {week, total}. Displayed as "X scans this week · Y all-time" pill in UniversalShareModal QR view (home header QR / Card QR). Tested: dedupe, self-view exclusion, UI render all verified on preview.
- **Contact Picker 50-Cap Fix (June 2026)**: All contact pickers previously loaded only the first 50 contacts and filtered locally — contacts beyond 50 were unfindable (user hit this on Voice Note picker). New shared hook `/app/frontend/hooks/useContactSearch.ts` does debounced server-side search (mirrors dialer's proven pattern; handles numeric queries + multi-word names). Wired into 9 pickers: home.tsx (ContactActionModal call picker, action picker/Voice Note, Send-a-Card picker), quick-send/photo.tsx, touchpoints/add-task.tsx, tasks/new.tsx, inbox.tsx new-conversation, contact/new.tsx referral, contact/[id].tsx referral. Verified: contact #62 "jimmy" found via Voice Note picker; clearing search restores default list.
- **"What to Send" Draft Sheet (June 2026)**: Home "Do This Next" / My-3 taps previously dumped users on the contact page with no guidance. Now they open a bottom sheet showing full "Why now" reason + an AI-drafted ready-to-send text (GET /api/home/draft/{user_id}/{contact_id}?reason=<reason_key> in home_intelligence.py, gpt-5.2 via Emergent LLM key, per-reason template fallbacks). Buttons: "Use This Message" (opens thread with composer prefilled, marks My-3 complete), "Different Message" (regenerate), "View Contact". Done My-3 cards still open contact page. Verified e2e on preview: sheet, AI draft (~2s), thread prefill all working.
- **Inbox tab reorder (June 2026)**: Waiting chip moved next to Hot (Hot → Waiting → All → AI → Active → Closed) per user request. Verified on preview.
- **Em-dash ban (June 2026)**: User NEVER wants em/en dashes in AI output (dead AI giveaway). Hard filter `no_em_dash()` in /app/backend/utils/text_sanitize.py (em/en dash → comma; digit ranges → hyphen) applied to EVERY AI text path: ai_reply.py (auto-reply + 2 follow-up writers), ai_campaigns.py (generate/reply/preview), messages.py (suggested reply), chat_widget.py, jessie_service.py, ai_outreach_service.py (suggestions + fallbacks), home_intelligence.py draft (+ prompt rule + fallbacks cleaned), va_profiles.py preview, home.tsx frontend fallback. RULE FOR FUTURE AGENTS: any new AI text generation MUST wrap output in no_em_dash(). Verified: unit tests + 3 live draft generations all clean.
- **Draft Everywhere / Write It on Touchpoints (June 2026)**: Draft sheet extracted to shared /app/frontend/components/DraftMessageSheet.tsx (home.tsx now uses it too). Touchpoints task cards gained a gold "Write It" button (Call | Write It | Text) that opens the sheet with reason 'touchpoint' (or birthday/anniversary by task type) + task title/description passed as `context` to the draft endpoint (new optional param). Backend: DRAFT_BRIEFS/DRAFT_FALLBACKS gained 'touchpoint' key. Verified e2e on preview: overdue task "Bud" → context-aware draft referencing his 2024 Tahoe; home sheet regression OK.
- **Auto-Complete on Send (June 2026)**: Draft sheet passes task_id → thread ?taskId=; thread/[id].tsx completes the task (PATCH /tasks action=complete, once via ref) after a successful send (both personal-SMS and regular flows). ALSO FIXED pre-existing bug: opening /thread/{contact_id} set actualConversationId to the contact id because GET /messages/thread/{contact_id} returns [] (200) — threads showed "No messages yet" despite history and sends failed "Conversation not found". Fix: if no messages AND conversation info 404s, fall through to find-or-create conversation. Verified e2e: Bud's history loaded, message sent, task auto-completed in DB.
- **Banned Words (June 2026)**: New persona.banned_words field (UserPersona model + persona wizard step 3 UI box, red label). Enforced two ways: injected into AI clone prompt + hard-stripped via strip_banned()/clean_ai_text(user_id) in utils/text_sanitize.py, applied at ai_reply (3), ai_campaigns (3), messages suggest, jessie, outreach, home draft. Verified: filter strips phrases (3/3 clean live drafts), settings box renders + loads saved value.
- **My VA rename + go-to phrases optional (June 2026)**: "Virtual Assistant" renamed to "My VA" everywhere (VA screen header, card name, Hub menu, my-profile button, persona wizard link). custom_phrases REMOVED from VA completeness scoring (now X of 11 — user's real missing item was go-to phrases, NOT payment link; payment link never counted). Root cause of user deleting phrases: AI overused them — clone prompt now says use sparingly, never force. persona.tsx labels go-to phrases "(optional)". Verified on preview.
- **Global iOS Dynamic Type cap (June 2026)**: User's iPhone large system font blew up layouts everywhere ("Next" wrapping to "Nex/t", phone numbers wrapping). Text.defaultProps is DEAD in RN 0.81/React 19. Fix: custom Babel plugin /app/frontend/babel-plugin-max-font.js registered in new /app/frontend/babel.config.js (babel-preset-expo + plugin) — injects maxFontSizeMultiplier={1} (user chose full 1.0 lock: app ignores phone text-size entirely) into EVERY <Text>/<TextInput>/<Animated.Text> at compile time; explicit props and spreads still win. All older per-element maxFontSizeMultiplier props (leads, home, dialer, report-bug, bug-reports, date-recipients, inventory, NotificationBell) removed via sed so nothing overrides the global 1.0. Verified via babel transform test + web smoke + home renders post-edit. NOTE: babel config changes require Metro cache clear (rm -rf node_modules/.cache) + frontend restart. Real effect only visible on iOS device after eas update.
- **Global keyboard dismiss (June 2026, user: "no way to push the keyboard down, noticed in several spots")**: second compile-time Babel plugin `/app/frontend/babel-plugin-keyboard.js` (registered after max-font in babel.config.js; app/ + components/ only, never node_modules): vertical ScrollView/FlatList/SectionList get `keyboardDismissMode="on-drag"` (swipe pushes the keyboard down); single-line `<TextInput>` get `returnKeyType="done"` (which also makes RN build its native Done toolbar for number-pad/phone-pad/decimal-pad keyboards, see RCTTextInputComponentView setDefaultInputAccessoryView); multiline `<TextInput>` are rewritten to `<KeyboardDoneWrap render={id => <TextInput inputAccessoryViewID={id} …/>} />` (`components/common/KeyboardDoneWrap.tsx`: iOS `InputAccessoryView` "Done" bar, unique nativeID per mount so lists work; null on Android/web). Explicit props / spreads always win; `horizontal` scrollers and inputs with their own `inputAccessoryViewID` are skipped. Verified: transform over all 419 tsx files (100 wrapped, 341 on-drag, 450 done, 0 errors), web bundle builds and typing works. REAL EFFECT ONLY ON iOS DEVICE after `eas update`. Babel changes need `rm -rf node_modules/.cache` + frontend restart. Don't hand-add Done buttons to individual inputs anymore.

- **P1 (SAVED FOR LATER, user request June 2026) "Number Health" / Twilio spam-label protection**: admin screen + auto-enrollment so rep and shared-inbox numbers never show "Spam Likely". Layers in order: (1) Trust Hub Business Profile (EIN, address, authorized rep, https site; user enters once in Twilio Trust Hub), (2) SHAKEN/STIR "A" attestation by assigning numbers to the profile, (3) CNAM per number (15-char caller name), (4) **Voice Integrity** bundle = the actual spam remediation (registers numbers with First Orion/TNS/Hiya for AT&T/Verizon/T-Mobile; clears labels in 24-48 h; per-number monthly fee), (5) optional Branded Calling Basic/Enhanced (name/logo on mobile; needs LOA; beta; regulation id RNca63d1066fbd5e44eac02d0b3cf6d019 v13), (6) behavior hygiene signals (short-call ratio <30 s, unanswered ratio, volume bursts per number, DNC) surfaced before carriers flag, (7) SMS side = A2P 10DLC brand + campaign registration (separate). Build plan: on number provisioning call Trust Hub API to assign number to Business Profile -> CNAM -> add to Voice Integrity bundle (`POST /v1/TrustProducts/{sid}/ChannelEndpointAssignments`); Number Health screen (Hub > Account Management, super_admin/store_manager) with per-number registration status + risk flags; monthly reminder to spot-check freecallerregistry.com; release/quarantine numbers when a rep leaves instead of reassigning. Docs: twilio.com/docs/voice/spam-monitoring-with-voiceintegrity, /docs/voice/branded-calling/us-basic, /docs/trust-hub/registrations.

- P1 Shared Inboxes Phase E: quick-send multiple tags / editable message / drop into inbox.
- **Inbox = Team wiring (June 2026, user: "5-6 people checked on the Website Inbox but only 3 available in the workflow; I can't think through lead source -> workflow -> inbox; bucket it in one place") - DONE, tested (backend `tests/inbox_team_wiring.py` 34/34 + testing_agent iteration_320 backend 8/8 + frontend 100%; regressions lead_flows_e2e 22/22, test_shared_inboxes_api 15/15, inbox_routing_matrix 60/61 where 10c is a time-of-day flake: web-form intake text is held after 20:00 Denver)**. ROOT CAUSE: three different "team" definitions. Inbox picker (super admin) = every user on the platform; lead source reps = store members + org admins; lead flow reps = store members only (`store_reps`). People invited at org level (store_id None) or homed on another store were checkable on the inbox but invisible to the ladder, and reps not in the source's static `workflow_user_ids` could not even see its lead queue. FIX: (1) `services/team_scope.py::eligible_people(db, store_id, me, inbox_ids?, include_everyone?)` = store members (store_id/store_ids) + the store's org admins + members of the store's active shared inboxes + caller, each row `via: [store|inbox|org|you]`, `on_team`; `include_everyone` appends every other active user with `on_team False` (super/org admins only). Used by `lead_flows.store_reps`, `GET /lead-sources/{id}/reps` (now also returns `inbox {id,name,members}`), `GET /inboxes/members/options?store_id=&inbox_id=` (returns `store_name`, `on_team`, `via`). `link_to_store(db, user_ids, store_id)`: saving an inbox links new members to the inbox's store (no store -> `store_id`, other home store -> `$addToSet store_ids`, super admins untouched); create/update responses carry `linked_to_store` (toast "N added to <store>'s team"). (2) `services/inboxes.INBOX_TOKEN = "@inbox"` may appear in `call_attempts[].user_ids` / `workflow_user_ids` on a source or flow = "everyone on the inbox"; `apply_inbox` (runs at intake AFTER `apply_flow`, plus in `lead_queue._store_sources` and the release push) resolves it to the inbox's current members via `resolve_team`, ALWAYS unions the inbox members into `workflow_user_ids` (notify + claim pool; the ladder alone decides whose phone rings), stamps `inbox_member_ids`; `source_member_ids` (lead queue visibility) includes them; `normalize_attempts` drops stray tokens (source with no inbox); `summarize`/`_names` label the token "everyone on the inbox"; `contact_match.source_owner_ids` skips tokens. PATCH /lead-sources/{id} with `team_id` now also sets `inbox_id` so both paths agree. (3) `services/inbox_leads.py` + endpoints (manage rights): `GET /api/inboxes/{id}/leads` -> {sources[{id,name,kind,direct,lead_count,flow,contact_mode,routing,notify_everyone,ringing{kind everyone|some|others|none|text_only, attempts, names, missing, dynamic, no_cell}, website_default, website_pages}], other_sources[{id,name,lead_count,inbox_name}], team, checklist{number, members, members_without_number, members_without_cell, sources, ringing_ok, ai_mode}}; `POST /{id}/sources {source_id}` (sets inbox_id+team_id; 400 direct/missing, 403 other store unless super admin), `DELETE /{id}/sources/{sid}`, `POST /{id}/sources/{sid}/ring-everyone` (adds @inbox to attempt 1 of the attached flow, synced to every source using it, else of the source's own ladder; forces contact_mode text_and_call). FRONTEND: inbox editor `/inboxes/[id]?tab=leads` new **Leads** tab (`components/inbox/InboxLeadsTab.tsx`: checklist `inbox-check-number|team|sources|ringing|jessi`, `inbox-source-direct`, `inbox-sources-empty`, per source `inbox-source-{id}`, `inbox-source-ringing-{id}`, gold `inbox-source-ring-all-{id}` (only text_and_call sources not already ringing everyone), `inbox-source-open-{id}` (flow editor or source page), `inbox-source-remove-{id}`, `inbox-point-source-toggle` + `inbox-point-source-{id}`); Team tab `MemberPicker` grouped "ON <STORE> · N" with `inbox-members-select-all` ("Everyone on the team" / "Clear all") and, for on_team=false rows (super/org admins), collapsible `inbox-members-show-others` + `inbox-members-search` ("Adding someone here also puts them on <store>'s team"); new-inbox hint `inbox-new-steps`, Create lands on `?tab=leads`. Inbox tab > Team Inbox chip row: gold `inbox-rules-btn` ("Manage inboxes" -> /inboxes, or "Rules & team" -> /inboxes/{id}?tab=leads when an inbox chip is selected). `LeadCallLadder` prop `inboxLabel` -> gold chip `ladder-{i}-rep-inbox` "Everyone on <label>" toggling @inbox (+ hint); flow editor passes "the source's inbox" and the text-only push list has `lead-flow-notify-inbox`; lead source page passes "the <inbox> inbox" when the source is in one, shows `workflow-inbox-note` (N people pinged + Open) and its TEAM radios became "LANDS IN SHARED INBOX" with `source-inbox-none` / `source-inbox-{id}`. Ship: backend Deploy + `eas update`.

UI `components/team/VoiceCoverageCard.tsx` (`voice-coverage-card`, `-enrolled`, `-calls`, `-verified`, `-mismatched`, `-rep-{id}`, `-rep-voice-{id}`, `-rep-calls-{id}`, `-toggle`, `-offline` when Eagle is down) on `app/admin/manage-team.tsx` under Add Team Member; hidden on 403.
  - DUTCH SPEED TO LEAD: `lead_flows.DUTCH_FLOW_TEXTS` + `localized_texts(source, locale)` (a Dutch store whose intake/after_hours/no_answer text still equals the English DEFAULT_FLOW gets the Dutch default at send time; edited or blank texts kept) applied in `lead_intake._fire_intake_workflow` and `lead_flows.on_ladder_exhausted` using `loc.store_locale(db, source.store_id or organization_id)`; `lead_sources.hydrate_intake_text(..., lang="nl")` fallbacks daar / de auto / het team; `ai_reply` hot-topic hold line is Dutch for a Dutch rep ("Goede vraag, dat check ik even en dan kom ik bij je terug."); `va_prompt.build_text(..., locale)` appends `loc.language_rule(locale)` (store locale) so the 4-layer VA writes Dutch. Already Dutch before: whisper, claim prompts, LLM first text.
  - EMAIL SHOPS: `services/email_shops.py` = text_shops twin with `mode: "email"`: `configured()` needs RESEND_API_KEY + INBOUND_EMAIL_DOMAIN (else the shop fails `not_configured` with the setup hint and shop-now/demo return 503); shopper From = `"{persona name} <{first.last}@{SHOP_EMAIL_DOMAIN or SENDER_EMAIL domain}>"` (verified with a REAL Resend send to delivered@resend.dev), `reply_to = shop+{session}@INBOUND_EMAIL_DOMAIN`; `opening_email` LLM JSON {subject, say}; `handle_inbound(db, sid, from, subject, body, email_id)` (dedupes by email_id, swallows replies to finished shops); `resend_webhooks.ingest_received_email` routes `shop+<24hex>@` in To/CC to it FIRST (`ems.session_id_from`), status "shop"; shopper replies after 3-15 min (`THINK_S`), `reply_if_due` sends "Re: subject"; `sweep` (in `run_due_calls`): stale 20 min, no reply 24 h -> `_grade_no_reply` (0%, `em.noreply.*` i18n, fail_reason "No reply to the email in 24 hours"), MAX 72 h, MAX_EXCHANGES 6; speed criteria `email_first` (30 min, critical, w2) / `email_pace` (2 h) via `speed_card/apply_speed`, `grader_transcript` starts with SUBJECT:. `scripts._customer_system(channel="email")` + `grade_session` picks tx/ems by mode, `scorecards._grader_prompt(channel="email")`. `mystery_shops`: `MODES`, `mode_of(channel)`, `mode_q(mode)` (same-mode busy checks everywhere), `create_shop_call` stores `rep_email`, `plan_month` schedules `plan.email_per_month` only to people with an email, `serialize_call`/`public_score` `channel: email` + `subject` + `rep_email`, report summary `email_shops/email_no_reply/avg_first_email_reply_s`, scorecard SMS intro `sms.intro_email`. Router: `PersonBody.email`/`DemoBody.email` (validated by `_email()`), `_client_fields` plan.email_per_month, `POST /calls/{sid}/end` ends text or email. UI: PeopleTab `person-email` field + `person-email-shop-{id}` (only when the person has an email), DemoShopSheet `demo-channel-email` + `demo-email`, CallsTab/ReportView/shop-score render channel email (mail icon, `em.badge`, `status.emailing`, `rep.emailed`, 30-min green threshold), `shared.isThread/channelIcon/channelWord`. SETUP FOR PRODUCTION (user): in Resend enable Receiving on a subdomain (e.g. reply.imonsocial.com, add its MX record), set `INBOUND_EMAIL_DOMAIN=reply.imonsocial.com` (+ optional `RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET`) in the deployed backend env, add a Resend webhook for `email.received` -> `https://app.imonsocial.com/api/webhooks/resend/inbound`. Then add people's work emails under People and tap Email shop.

Written at recording-complete in `twilio_webhooks._transcribe_and_save` onto `call_logs` (next to the raw `voice_id` check) and the thread `messages` call_log doc; `messages.py` `/thread` serializer passes `voice_verified`; `voice_notes.py` list passes `voice_verified` from `voice_id.verified` (in-person recorded conversations). Fixes while wiring: `voice_id._lib_kwargs()` falls back to pveagle's generic `lib/raspberry-pi/cortex-a76-aarch64/libpv_eagle.so` on non-RPi ARM Linux (pveagle 3.0.2 only auto-detects x86_64 + RPi CPU parts, raised `Unsupported CPU 0xd4f`); `score()` now uses the 3.x recognizer API (`eagle.process` on 1 s chunks, `min_process_samples` 16384; there is no `frame_length` on the recognizer, only on the profiler); `voice_id.available()` (cached) gates `summary()`/`enroll_user()` so a bad key or unsupported CPU shows `not_configured` + reason instead of a failed enrollment every call; `lab.list_features` sets voice_interview.needs dynamically. PRODUCTION: the deployed backend env must also get `PICOVOICE_ACCESS_KEY` (Deploy -> environment) or Voice ID stays "coming soon" there.

INBOUND ROUTING (`ai_reply.queue_ai_reply`): `_va = va_prompt.routing(db, assigned_user_id)` (None while the flag is off for that rep = old behaviour) -> FACT_SIGNALS/FINANCE_SIGNALS come from the industry; `vehicle_ok` forced False for non-automotive; `fact_answers = va_prompt.match_facts(msg, facts)` (shared 5-char token, stop words stripped) -> if a fact matches a fact question the hold is skipped, Jessi answers with `facts_block` in the user prompt, conversation flagged needs_assistance + push "Answered from your facts - <name>"; no match -> canned "Good question, let me check" + `ai_paused_for_human` as before. API `routers/va.py` (prefix /va, require_user): `GET /config` {available, industry{key,label,source,store_name}, can_pick_industry, industries, scenarios, hold, safe, tone, facts{store,mine,store_name,can_edit_store}}, `PUT /industry` (409 when the store has an industry), `GET /facts` {store[], mine[], store_id, store_name, can_edit_store, max 60}, `POST /facts {text, scope mine|store}` (store = MANAGER_ROLES on that store, 400 no store, 403 rep, 400 <3 chars, 240 max), `PUT /facts/{id}`, `DELETE /facts/{id}?scope=`. INTERVIEW: `interview.BASE_TOPICS` 9 (nickname, years, hometown, family, hobbies, why, texting, never_say, fun_fact) + `topics(industry)` inserts the industry `slot` as the 6th topic (automotive `vehicles` "What you drive", others `interests` with the pack's question/label); session stores `industry` at start; `MAX_MINUTES 6`, `WRAP_AFTER_MINUTES 4`, `MAX_REP_TURNS 24`; greeting says "about five minutes" and "your role" (no "store"); `PERSONA_STR` dropped custom_phrases/greeting_style/signature (extract prompt no longer asks for them; `vehicles` blank unless the slot is vehicles); `serialize` returns `topics_total` 10, `labels` merged with the slot label, `industry`. - **Onboarding Voice Interview + Voice ID + Test Lab (Sep 16 2026, user: "Jessi calls the new rep, 10 min two-way interview, auto-builds VA bio/persona/card, learns their voice so anywhere they talk we know it's them"; then "add a link to my profile that lets me test this, don't update my profile, add a test area with new things so I can go through it before I push to anyone else") - DONE, tested (`tests/interview_sim.py` full ConversationRelay websocket sim incl. dry run + lab flags ALL OK; testing_agent iteration_326 + 327 backend 14/14, frontend 100%).** NEW `services/interview.py` + `routers/interview.py` (collection `interview_sessions`, separate from roleplay_sessions so practice stats stay clean). Flow: `POST /api/interview/start {dry_run?}` rings `users.phone` from the rep's twilio/mvpline number with `record=True, recording_track="inbound", recording_channels="mono"` (= the rep's voice ONLY), TwiML `/api/interview/call/twiml/{sid}?t=` -> ConversationRelay (Google en-US-Journey-F, Deepgram nova-3, welcomeGreeting = Jessi intro + first question), ws `/api/interview/relay/{sid}/{token}`; `relay_turn` runs gpt-5.2 with `_system()` = 17 `TOPICS` (nickname, years, hometown, family, hobbies, vehicles, specialties, ideal_customer, story, motto, tone, humor, phrases, never_say, fun_fact, why_me, greeting), tracks `covered[]`, wraps at 9 min / hard stop 12 min (~1.5 s per turn). `finalize` (after/status/websocket close, first wins) -> `build`: `extract()` = one LLM JSON pass -> PERSONA_STR/LIST/ENUM fields + `highlights`; `apply()` sets `persona.<k>` for every non-empty field, `users.title` from professional_identity when empty, `persona_interviewed_at`, onboarding_complete when photo+bio; push "Your VA is ready" -> `/interview/review?session=`. `save_recording` stores `interview/{sid}/rep.mp3` then `voice_id.enroll_user`. Statuses dialing/live/ending/building/completed/failed/abandoned (<3 rep turns). API: `GET /status` {session, voice, phone (masked), can_call, available, is_super_admin, persona_filled, interviewed_at}, `GET/POST /sessions/{sid}` + `/hangup`, `/rebuild` (re-run extraction), `/apply` (dry run -> profile), `DELETE /voice`. NEW `services/voice_id.py` = Picovoice Eagle (`pveagle` in requirements): `to_pcm16k` via imageio_ffmpeg, `enroll` (min_enrollment_chunks 3, needs 100%), `score` (median after warm-up), `enroll_user` -> `users.voice_id {status enrolled|partial|failed|not_configured, profile Binary, percent, enrolled_at, source, seconds}`, `identify/verify_user/rep_channel`. Hooks: `twilio_webhooks` dual-channel call transcription lets `rep_channel()` override the direction-based left/right speaker assignment and stamps `call_logs.voice_id`; `voice_notes._process_voice_note` stamps `voice_notes.voice_id {score, verified}` in the background. **`PICOVOICE_ACCESS_KEY` in backend/.env is EMPTY: everything reports `not_configured` ("Voice ID: coming soon") until the user pastes a key from console.picovoice.ai; Eagle itself is UNVERIFIED on real audio until then (threshold `VOICE_ID_THRESHOLD` default 0.6, calibrate).** TEST LAB: `services/lab.py` FEATURES registry (`voice_interview`) + `db.settings {key: lab_features}`; `GET/PUT /api/lab/features[/{key}] {status: lab|live}` (super_admin). Feature is `lab` by default: the InterviewCard on `/my-profile` and `/settings/virtual-assistant` renders ONLY when live (`status.available`); reps get 403 on start until released; dry runs (`dry_run: true`, super admin only) build the write-up but `applied: false` until `/apply`. UI: `components/profile/InterviewCard.tsx` (`interview-card`, `interview-start-btn`, `interview-status`, `interview-hangup`, `interview-review-btn`, `interview-redo-btn`, `interview-voice-pill`, `interview-add-phone`; `dryRun` prop = "Test the interview"), `app/interview/review.tsx` (highlights, per-field cards, transcript bubbles; preview state `interview-apply-btn` "Save this to my profile", `interview-rebuild-btn`), `app/admin/test-lab.tsx` (`lab-feature-*`, `lab-status-*`, `lab-more-*`, embedded dry-run card, `lab-release-*` Switch with confirm), My Profile `profile-test-lab-link` (super_admin) + Hub Internal Operations "Test Lab" tile. RULE: new features should be added to `lab.FEATURES` and gated with `lab.visible()` so Forest can try them first. Sim: `cd /app/backend && python tests/interview_sim.py [--keep]`. Ship: backend Deploy + `eas update`. Forest tests on production via My Profile > Test Lab > Call me (his real phone).

- **Text (SMS) Mystery Shops (Sep 2026, user: "Let Jessi shop a dealership over SMS from the shop number and grade the reply speed and quality like a call") - DONE, tested (`tests/test_text_shops.py` 4/4: 3 in-process with faked Twilio/LLM + 1 real server e2e through `/webhooks/twilio/incoming`, LLM shopper reply and real grading; regressions contact_card, carrier_decline, shop_report_departments, dutch_pack, uk_pack all pass; UI screenshot-verified on QA Jeep).** NEW `services/text_shops.py`. Sessions are the same `roleplay_sessions` docs with `mode: "text"` (`kind: mystery_shop`, `direction` forced inbound); `serialize_call` adds `channel: call|text` and `text: {replies, customer_texts, first_reply_s, avg_reply_s, max_reply_s, waiting_since, end_reason}`. FLOW: `place_shop_call` branches to `tx.start_text_shop` (one live thread per (shop number, cell) pair, otherwise reschedules +3h); the opener is written by the LLM from the persona (`_customer_system(..., channel="text")`, SMS-style rules) and sent from the client's shop number; rep replies hit `/webhooks/twilio/incoming` FIRST (`tx.handle_inbound` matches `from_number`=To + `rep_phone`=From, status live, appends a rep turn with `delay_s` measured from the shopper's last text, then `schedule_reply` waits a human 20-75s before `reply_if_due` (atomic `text_lock`, LLM reply, sends, `ended` -> status `ending` -> `finish`)); `tx.sweep` runs inside `run_due_calls` every 2 min: lost delayed replies (last turn rep > 150s), no reply for `REPLY_WINDOW_MIN` 240 min -> `finish(no_reply|no_reply_mid)`, > `MAX_HOURS` 24 -> `expired`, stuck `ending` -> grade. GRADING: `scr.grade_session` for text sessions uses `tx.speed_card` (dept scorecard + 2 measured criteria `text_first` "Replied to the first text within 5 minutes" CRITICAL w2, `text_pace` "Kept every reply under 15 minutes" w1; nl copies) and `tx.grader_transcript` ("REP: [replied after 3 min] ..."), `sc.grade_with_ai(..., channel="text")` (prompt says SMS thread, speed criteria are system-measured), then `tx.apply_speed` overrides those two results from the clock; eval carries `channel`, `text_stats`. NO REPLY = the result: `_grade_no_reply` writes a `graded_by: system` eval with every criterion failed, score 0, summary "X never replied. The shopper texted at 2:14 PM and gave up after 4 hours of silence", session `outcome: no_reply` (OUTCOME_LABEL). Scorecard SMS uses `sms.intro_text` / `sms.link_text` ("those texts just now were a practice shop"). PLAN: `client.plan.text_per_month {dept: n}` (`plan_text_per_month`, `_client_fields`, ClientSheet "TEXT SHOPS PER MONTH" row, proposal `terms.text_per_month` + BillingTab fields + `prop.what.text` sentence + `prop.tile.texts` tile); `plan_month` tops up phone and text quotas separately (returns `{..., "text": {dept: n}}`); `run_due_calls` busy check is same-mode. API: `POST /shop-clients/{cid}/calls/shop-now {target_id, channel: "text"}` (409 if a text shop is live for them), `POST /shop-clients/demo {..., channel}`, `POST /shop-clients/calls/{sid}/end` (live/ending text only -> graded now), `GET /calls/{sid}` transcript_turns carry `delay_s`; `public/shop-score` adds `channel`, `text`, `transcript_turns`. UI: PeopleTab outlined `person-text-shop-{id}` next to Shop now; DemoShopSheet `demo-channel-call|text` chips; CallsTab rows show chatbubbles icon + "· text", live line "waiting on Bud for N min" + `shop-call-end-{id}` "End & grade", completed "first reply 3 min · 4 replies", detail sheet `shop-call-text-badge` + `shop-call-speed` + per-bubble "replied after N min"; STATUS gained `ending` ("Wrapping up"), StatusChip `channel` prop shows "Texting"; ReportView `report-stat-text` (avg first reply · N unanswered) + row first-reply line; public score page `score-text-speed` + `score-thread` bubbles; `[id].tsx` header and plan button append "+ N sales by text". Ship: backend Deploy + `eas update`.

- **AI shopper says numbers like a human (June 2026, user: "they said one three two dot eight nine instead of a hundred and thirty-two and eighty-nine cents") - DONE, tested (`pytest tests/test_speech.py` 6/6; backend restarted clean with num2words)**: NEW `services/speech.py` `speakable(text)` (deterministic verbalizer, idempotent): phone numbers -> spoken digit groups "four three five, two seven five, nine eight two nine"; `$132.89` / bare `132.89` -> "one hundred thirty-two dollars and eighty-nine cents" (`$0.50` -> "fifty cents", `$45k`, `$2M`); `6.9%` -> "six point nine percent"; `9:30 am` / `4:05 pm` / `10:00` -> "nine thirty AM" / "four oh five PM" / "ten o'clock"; `2022` (1950-2039) -> "twenty twenty-two"; `45,000` -> "forty-five thousand"; `45k`; `3rd` -> "third"; `4.5` -> "four point five"; plain ints -> words; 10+ digit ids -> digits. Uses `num2words` (added to requirements.txt). APPLIED to everything a TTS voice reads in `services/scripts.py`: `relay_twiml` welcomeGreeting, `relay_turn` say, `relay_nudge`, the shop announcement `<Say>`, prelude line, hangup/announce TwiML. Stored transcripts keep the LLM's original text (digits) for readability. PROMPT: `NUMBERS_RULE` appended to `_customer_system` for live calls (say numbers as words, examples). ALSO applied to the lead-call engine: `lead_call_engine._say` wraps every spoken line (whisper 'Interested in twenty twenty-two Tahoe under forty-five thousand dollars', callback numbers in comments, 'Press one to claim'); the `<Dial>` number is untouched. Add them to the store under Team Members or to the inbox's Team tab.", testid `ladder-team-note`) and off-team handling: team chips first, selected off-team people shown with "not on this team", a dashed `ladder-{i}-show-others` chip ("N others on the platform" / "Hide others") opening `ladder-{i}-others` with `ladder-{i}-others-search` (40 max). (4) Diagnostic: Tools footer shows `build-stamp` (utils/buildStamp.ts: "update <8 chars> · <date time>" from expo-updates, "store build (no OTA update applied yet)", blank on web) so "I updated but see the old thing" is a 10-second check. NOTE for support: app.json has `checkAutomatically ON_LOAD` + `fallbackToCacheTimeout 0`, so an `eas update` downloads on the first launch and APPLIES ON THE SECOND launch: fully close and reopen the app twice. - **Flow editor showed only 3 people (June 2026, PRODUCTION report: "still only seeing 3 people as options" on flow "Ring everyone, then the manager") - FIXED, tested (`pytest tests/test_flow_reps_pool.py` 1/1 cross-store repro; regressions inbox_team_wiring 34/34, lead_flows_e2e 22/22, test_lead_flows_api_regression + test_inbox_team_wiring_review 18/18; QA flow editor renders the full QA team)**: ROOT CAUSE: `app/lead-flows/[id].tsx` took `reps` from `GET /lead-flows` (the library), whose pool is `store_reps(caller's store)`. When an admin whose home store is the iMOS store (App Reviewer, Kira Dark, Forest) edits a flow used by a dealership's source, the dealership's reps and its Website-inbox members never appear (only the dynamic "Everyone on the source's inbox" chip covered them at ring time). FIX: `routers/lead_flows.py::_flow_reps(db, flow, me)` = union of `eligible_people` for the flow's store AND every store of a lead source using the flow, with `inbox_ids` = those sources' `inbox_id`/`team_id` + every active inbox on those stores (via lists merged); no store anywhere -> the admin all-users list. `GET /lead-flows/{id}` now returns `reps` + `store_hours` (flow's store); the editor prefers `f.data.reps`/`store_hours` and falls back to the library. Ship: backend Deploy + `eas update`.

- **Shop calls hung up on real people (June 2026, USER BUG "it double beeps and hangs up after I answer and start talking") - FIXED, verified with a LIVE call to the user's cell (17 turns, graded)**: `place_shop_call` used Twilio AMD (`machine_detection="Enable", machine_detection_timeout=12`), which (a) holds the answered call SILENT until AMD decides and (b) labels any greeting longer than ~2.4s of speech as `AnsweredBy=machine_start`. A normal dealership greeting ("Thanks for calling, this is Forest, how can I help you?") trips it every time, and `relay_twiml` then returned `<Hangup/>` + `record_outcome(..., "voicemail")` = dead air, carrier disconnect tones, dropped call. FIX (user chose "no auto voicemail detection at all, always let the call run"): AMD params removed from `place_shop_call`; the `AnsweredBy` machine/fax branch deleted from `relay_twiml` (relay now connects the instant the line is answered, machine label or not). Voicemail is no longer auto-detected or auto-retried for answered calls: a shop that reaches voicemail records a transcript and gets graded, so watch the transcript. Pre-answer outcomes (no-answer / busy / failed / canceled) still retry via `/status` + `reconcile_dialing`. Practice calls never used AMD, so they were never affected. ALSO FIXED while verifying: `services/scorecards.py::_parse_json` raised on the grader's occasional escaped-quote / prose-wrapped JSON (real call came back `score_pct: None`, adherence only) - it is now lenient (fence strip + outer-brace regex + unescape fallback) with one retry prompt in `grade_with_ai`. Tests updated: `tests/mystery_shop_e2e.py` now asserts a machine label still returns `ConversationRelay` and leaves `attempt_history` empty (ALL OK); `tests/relay_roleplay_sim.py` ALL OK. TESTING TIP (how the live call was verified in preview): temporarily set backend `.env` `PUBLIC_FACING_URL` to the preview host so Twilio's TwiML/status/websocket callbacks hit preview code (wss through the preview ingress works; a 403 on the relay websocket just means "session not found", Starlette's pre-accept close), place the call, then REVERT `PUBLIC_FACING_URL` to https://app.imonsocial.com and restart backend. Ship: backend Deploy (production still runs the old hang-up code until then).

Starter `inbound_sales_call` is inbound (set via `$set` in `ensure_starters`, all others outbound); `script_direction(s)` falls back by slug for legacy store copies; `serialize_script` returns it; `ScriptBody.direction` accepted by create/update (`save_store_copy` whitelist); import LLM returns `direction`. PHONE: `relay_twiml` omits `welcomeGreeting` for inbound, `relay_setup` does not push the opening turn, and the FIRST rep prompt is answered instantly (no LLM) with `persona.opening_line` inside `customer_turn` (same rule for text mode: `start_session` returns `customer: null` for inbound). `_customer_system` gets `direction` ("YOU placed this call" vs "the salesperson called YOU"). `GET /roleplay/{sid}` + start responses include `direction`. - **"Application error" ~3 min into phone practice (June 2026, PRODUCTION report) - FIXED (root cause from Twilio Debugger alerts, read via monitor API with preview creds)**: two causes. (1) `customer_turn` forced `ended` at `MAX_TURNS = 14` rep exchanges and the prompt told the customer to end "past 12 exchanges"; on a live call that is ~3 minutes, so the customer said goodbye and ConversationRelay ended (`HandoffData customer_ended`, SessionDuration 190). (2) The `/after/{sid}` hangup TwiML used `<Say voice="Google.en-US-Journey-F">`, which Twilio rejects with 13520 "Say: Invalid text" -> the rep hears "an application error has occurred" instead of "Nice work...". FIX: phone mode has NO exchange cap; it ends only when the customer naturally wraps up (appointment set / declined for good / rep says goodbye) or at `PHONE_MAX_MINUTES = 15` (`PHONE_MAX_TURNS = 60` safety), where the prompt tells the customer to wrap up in one sentence and `ended` is forced. Text mode cap raised to `MAX_TURNS = 24`. `hangup_twiml` now uses `Polly.Joanna-Neural`. Verified: out-of-time session -> ended True, exchange-15 fresh call -> ended False, `relay_roleplay_sim.py` ALL OK. Needs backend Deploy to reach production. Debug tip: `client.monitor.v1.alerts.list(start_date=...)` + `.fetch()` shows `response_body`/`request_variables` for any Twilio "application error".

NEW FLOW/SOURCE FIELDS (FLOW_FIELDS + DEFAULT_FLOW + WorkflowConfig + serialize + clamps): `ring_delay_seconds` (0-300, default 0) and `returning_stale_days` (0-365, default 30; 0 = always their rep). ENGINE: `start_call_workflow` fires attempt 1 instantly only when ring_delay is 0; otherwise `next_attempt_at = now + delay` and the 15s `process_lead_call_jobs` tick fires it (job stamped `ring_delay_seconds`); a held first rung (deferred OR ring-delayed) is skipped when a human claimed or a rep already texted; routing kinds (`assigned`/`returning_owner`/`queue` in `claim_source`) no longer count as a human claim, so assigned reps still get rung. INTAKE: `lead_intake.returning_owner_verdict(db, contact, owner_id, days, now)` -> sold (tag Sold / sold_at / status sold) or any activity in N days (contact.created_at, owner<->contact conversations last_message_at + latest message, call_logs, non-view contact_events, completed tasks) keeps the owner; otherwise `owner_id=None` -> normal flow (round robin / queue + ladder), `returning_reopened {prev_owner_id, prev_owner_name, quiet_days, stale_days, reason}` stamped on inbound_leads + conversation, quiet `lead_reopened` notification (ACTION_TYPES "today"/Open, category leads) to the previous owner. TIMELINE: `timeline_for_conversation` returns `routing {kind, owner_name, ladder_skipped, reopened, prev_owner_name, quiet_days, stale_days, ring_delay_seconds}`; `LeadCallTimeline` says "Forest's customer · Sent straight to Forest: text only, phones did not ring" for returning-owner leads, adds rows "Returning customer of X, no sale and quiet N days: the team is working it" and "Text first, phones ring 30s later". UI: `LeadCallLadder` step 1 chips "Text first, ring after: Same moment / 15 / 30 / 45 / 60s" (`ladder-ring-delay-{s}`, flow editor only via `ringDelay`/`onRingDelayChange`), flow Settings "Returning customers" radios (`lead-flow-returning-always|stale`) + quiet-for chips 14/30/60/90 (`lead-flow-stale-{d}`); summaries updated. - **TIMEZONE RULE (June 2026, user: "push at 8am but task says 9am, time discrepancies through the app")**: ROOT CAUSE - two resolvers: call_followup used `resolve_user_tz` which preferred `user_schedules.timezone` (Forest's production schedule tz was a Central zone) while task reminder pushes/most of the app used `users.timezone` (device, Denver) -> "Try again tomorrow 9:00 AM" was 9:00 Central = 8:00 on his phone. FIX: `routers/user_schedule.py::resolve_user_tz` is THE ONLY resolver: `users.timezone` (phone, refreshed on password login, Face ID `/auth/refresh` {timezone}, and every app open via `/push/subscribe-native` {timezone}) -> schedule tz -> store tz -> America/Denver; 'UTC' = unknown. `set_device_timezone()` validates IANA names. `GET /schedule/me` returns the effective tz (`timezone_source: device`) and the Schedule screen labels it "taken from your phone". Also unified stray defaults: scheduler task reminders use the resolver, ai_outreach_service (was New_York), calendar.py Google events (was New_York) use resolver, lead_intake store default Chicago -> Denver. RULE FOR ANY NEW CODE: never read users.timezone / schedule tz directly for rep-local math; call `await resolve_user_tz(user_id)`. Store-hours logic stays on `stores.timezone`.
- **False "2x buyer" (June 2026, user: Mike Wolthuis is not a repeat buyer)**: ROOT CAUSE - three Sold paths each inferred "date_sold already exists => repeat" and `$inc sold_count`: sold-quick calls `date-sold` FIRST, then the congrats card with `tags:['Sold']` (congrats_cards.py) and the fallback `tags` PATCH both saw date_sold and incremented (+ pushed a bogus "Previous purchase" purchase_history entry). FIX: new `services/sales.py::record_sale(db, contact_id, sale_date, only_if_unsold)` is the ONLY writer of date_sold/sold_count/purchase_history (first | same-transaction [same day or recorded <7d ago, e.g. date correction] | repeat [archives previous sale incl. `prev_vehicle` stashed by the vehicle PATCH]). `date-sold` endpoint = the only place repeats are recorded; tag paths (tags PATCH, congrats card) call it with only_if_unsold=True. Startup migration `sold_count_repair_2026_09` (`repair_false_repeats`) removes same-day bogus history entries and recomputes sold_count (ran on preview: fixed 2; will fix Mike on production after Deploy). sold-quick fallback tag patch no longer wipes existing tags (merges Sold). Verified with a scripted sequence (first/skipped/skipped -> 1; date fix -> same; 400-day-old sale -> repeat 2; repair keeps legit repeats).
- **Blank full-size profile photo (June 2026, user: Chris Harley thumbnail opens black viewer)**: the header avatar renders `photo_thumbnail || photo_url || photo` (often legacy base64) while GalleryModal loads `/api/images/{photo_path}`; on production some CSV-imported contacts carry the migration sentinel `photo_path: "skipped_too_large"` / `"error:..."` (bulk migration skipped >3MB base64) -> `/api/images/skipped_too_large` 404 -> blank. FIX: `GET /contacts/{u}/{c}/photos/all` now treats sentinel/missing photo_path as invalid and SELF-HEALS: uploads the base64 `photo` to object storage on the spot, updates photo_path/thumb/avatar/photo_url/photo_thumbnail, returns the real URL (verified end to end on preview); else falls back to photo_url/photo_thumbnail/base64. Frontend GalleryModal: `onError` fallback chain full -> thumbnail_url -> `fallbackPhoto` (avatar source, passed from contact/[id].tsx). Ship: backend Deploy + eas update.

(2) CLAIM = CALL ME: `lead_sources.claim_lead` on a Text+Call source (or auto_call_on_claim) now calls `lead_call_engine.call_rep_now(conv, user)` -> rings the rep's cell (kind 'callback'); `/answer` webhook for the owner returns `twiml_connect` ("You claimed X. Press 1 to connect now"), `/claim` webhook bridges without re-claiming when claimed_by == user; other reps hear "already claimed". Creates a claimed job on the fly when no ladder job exists (text-only source). Response includes `calling_rep`. (3) New `POST /api/lead-sources/ring-me/{conversation_id}?user_id=` (self/manager; 409 if another rep owns it) used by the "Ring me & connect to this lead" gold button in `components/LeadCallTimeline.tsx` (card auto-opens when job active/exhausted; callback rows labelled "Rang X to connect (claimed in app)"; timeline API now returns `kind` per call). (4) Push copy: "Ringing your phone now: answer and press 1" vs "Store closed: ... your phone rings at 9:05 AM. Tap to text now" (no more "tap to claim" on Text+Call). (5) `LeadCallLadder` warns per attempt when a selected rep has no cell number (`ladder-{i}-no-phone`). Deferred-to-opening behaviour kept by design (user: "only ring during hours but call first thing next morning"). `_dial_rep` helper extracted from `_run_attempt`. Verified on preview with Twilio test creds: instant attempt within 2s, claim -> callback ring, TwiML paths, 409. Preview "Website" source ladder rings test user activation-tester (+15005550006).

RULES: a thread that arrives on a shared number stays on that number while worked (replies from rep/Jessi/campaigns go out from the inbox number via `services/inboxes.resolve_from_number`); first member to reply or tap Claim owns it (`send_message` auto-claims); known customers of an active rep keep texting that rep's line (`_existing_owner`); inbox close tag (default Sold) graduates the thread per `after_close` (move_to_rep -> rep's own number + bridge text, or stay); owner gets every alert, collaborators only when owner is off shift / silent 15 min (`escalate_silent_owners` scheduler). BACKEND `services/inboxes.py` + `routers/inboxes.py` (`/api/inboxes`, Bearer auth): GET list (counts open/unassigned/mine, can_manage, is_member, can_create), GET /numbers (manager; Twilio account numbers flagged available/taken), GET /members/options (managers = store/org scope, reps = own store + inbox co-members), POST/GET/PUT/DELETE/{id} (409 on number owned by a rep or other inbox; DELETE 409 while live convs unless ?force=true; every inbox owns a `kind: inbox_direct` lead source via `ensure_direct_source`), GET /{id}/conversations?view=all|unassigned|mine, GET /conversations/{conv}/ownership (owner, collaborators, history, `can{claim,assign,release,move,share,graduate}`, inboxes, members), POST /conversations/{conv}/claim|assign|release|move|collaborators|graduate (PermissionError->403, ValueError->400, double claim 409, graduate w/o personal number 409). Assignment engine `pick_assignee`: jump_ball | round_robin (on-shift, daily_cap) | weighted_round_robin. `twilio_webhooks` routes texts to a shared number through `handle_inbound`; `messages.get_conversations` uses `access_filter` (own + assigned + collaborator + unclaimed in member inboxes) and enriches `inbox_id/inbox_name/assigned_to_name/is_unassigned/is_mine/is_collaborator`; `/conversation/{id}/info` returns the same ownership fields. Every ownership action writes a `sender: 'system'` event message (thread renders as centered chip). FRONTEND: `components/inbox/{ownership.ts (API wrapper + helpers), OwnershipSheet.tsx, OwnershipPickers.tsx (RepPicker/InboxPicker/NoteConfirm), InboxThreadBanner.tsx, InboxBadge.tsx (badge + ClaimButton), SharedInboxPanel.tsx, InboxEditorParts.tsx}`. Thread (`thread/[id].tsx`): gold banner under header actions "<Inbox> inbox · Up for grabs | You have this | <First> has this" + Claim/Manage -> "Who's on this" sheet (LIVES IN, WHO HAS IT w/ I've got it / Give to someone / Hand off, ALSO ON THIS THREAD share/remove, JESSI row -> settings, MOVE IT release/move/graduate, HISTORY); also reachable from the '...' settings modal row `ownership-settings-row` on any thread (personal threads: share + manager move). Inbox tab: My Inbox rows show `InboxBadge` (colored dot + inbox · owner) + small Claim, new 'Up for grabs' filter chip (`inbox-tab-unassigned`, shown when the user has inboxes); Team Inbox segment = `SharedInboxPanel` (chips All inboxes / per-inbox w/ open counts, Everything / Up for grabs / Mine; rows w/ handoff note + Claim) when the user has any inbox, legacy team view otherwise. Hub > Manage > "Inboxes" (managers) and Internal Ops "Shared Inboxes" both -> `/inboxes` library (cards: number, stats, member avatars, routing/Jessi/close summary, New) and `/inboxes/[id]` editor (id 'new' creates): tabs Setup (name, description, color, number w/ Twilio list), Team (members incl. out-of-scope ones, routing option rows, weights for weighted, daily cap), Jessi (first reply w/ merge chips, ai_mode, VA name/training/rules/handoff), Closing (close tag, after_close, bridge text), Save/Delete (409 -> "Delete anyway"). Legacy `app/admin/shared-inboxes.tsx` left in place but no longer linked. DEMO DATA: `backend/tests/seed_inbox_demo.py` (idempotent, preview only; see test_credentials.md). - **Lead Flows Library (June 2026, Phase B) - DONE, tested (backend e2e 22/22 `backend/tests/lead_flows_e2e.py`, testing_agent iteration_305 backend 10/10 + frontend UI; blank-flow 400 bug found + fixed)**: CallRevu-style reusable "what happens when a lead comes in" playbooks. BACKEND `services/lead_flows.py` + `routers/lead_flows.py` (managers/admins only, `/api/lead-flows`): list (returns flows + store reps + store_hours + templates), POST (body or `template_key` = ring_all_then_manager | text_first_push_only | after_hours_jessi; templates pre-fill the store's reps/managers by role), GET/PUT/DELETE/{id} (DELETE 409 while attached unless `?force=true`), POST /{id}/duplicate, PUT /source/{source_id} {flow_id|null} attach/detach. MODEL: flow = Steps (call_attempts up to 6, each {user_ids, delay_seconds, delivery call|push}) + Settings (contact_mode, after_hours_mode, text window, caller_id_mode rep|store, auto_call_on_claim, notify_all_on_intake) + Automations (intake_text, after_hours_text [used instead of intake when store closed], no_answer_text, va_enabled, inquiry_context, tags_on_claim, tags_on_no_answer, exhausted_text_lead, exhausted_push_manager). "FLOW WINS": attaching MIRRORS the flow's FLOW_FIELDS onto lead_sources (flow_id/flow_name set) so every existing intake/ladder/queue path keeps reading source keys; saving a flow re-syncs all sources using it (`sync_flow_to_sources`, response `synced_sources`); `apply_flow()` re-merges at intake (process_inbound_lead), in the call engine (`_job_source`) and claim; PUT /lead-sources/{id}/workflow strips FLOW_FIELDS while attached; detach keeps the last copy. ENGINE: MAX_ATTEMPTS 4->6, per-attempt `delivery: push` (`_push_rep`, outcome 'pushed'), caller_id_mode honoured in `_from_number_for`, `_after_last_attempt` waits RING+10s then (if still unclaimed) pushes reps AND runs `on_ladder_exhausted` (no-answer text from conv.rep_phone/rep line/env number, tags, manager push+notification; lead stays in queue; actions stored on job.exhausted_actions and shown in LeadCallTimeline); `on_lead_claimed` (claim_lead, claim_and_call, phone press-1) adds tags_on_claim + runs tag workflows. FRONTEND: `app/lead-flows/index.tsx` (Hub > Leads > Lead Flows, managers: cards w/ summary, sources chips, Edit/Duplicate/Delete, template sheet), `app/lead-flows/[id].tsx` editor (Steps / Settings / Automations tabs, header Save, summary), `components/admin/LeadFlowPicker.tsx` on Lead Source > Response Workflow (Use a flow / Edit flow / Change / Detach; hides intake, inquiry, reps, contact mode, ladder, after-hours, auto-call, VA toggle while attached), `components/admin/LeadFlowSummary.tsx`, LeadCallLadder gained `max` + `allowPush` props. SAFETY NOTE for testing: the ring_all/after_hours templates pre-fill real store managers (Forest Ward has a real cell in preview) - never run a test lead through a flow that includes them; use Activation Tester (+15005550006) only. Ship: backend Deploy + `eas update`.
- **Image storage self-heal (June 2026, PRODUCTION report: photos missing on web, fine on native = device cache)**: prod `/api/images/*` all 404 while the objects exist (verified via preview key). `utils/image_storage._storage_request` re-inits a stale storage key on 401/403 for reads/lists too (uploads already did); `serve_image` logs the storage status and returns 503 when storage is unreachable; diagnostic `GET /api/images/_health` (key_configured, init_ok, sample_status). After deploy check `_health` in prod; if init fails there the deployment's EMERGENT_LLM_KEY env is wrong (Emergent Support).
- **Vanishing leads fix (June 2026, PRODUCTION report)**: root cause = leads for phone numbers that already exist as the rep's contacts route as `returning_owner` (claimed by the owner -> "Mine"); the customer's reply to the intake text landed in the OLD personal thread (webhook `find_one` on (rep_phone, contact_phone) returned the oldest), so the rep replied there, the lead thread had no "human reply", `process_returning_lead_escalations` auto-released it after 30 min, and a released lead was only visible if the source doc matched the manager's `store_id` -> gone from Leads. Fixes: (1) `lead_queue._lead_scope_or`: managers see every internet lead whose `conversation.store_id` is in their scope (`_me` builds `scope_store_ids` from store_id + store_ids + org stores for org_admin; super_admin `sees_everything`); reps also see leads released FROM them (`prev_owner_id`, set in `release_to_queue`); `_store_sources` uses the same scope. (2) `_owner_replied_elsewhere`: the owner's human reply to the same contact in a sibling thread counts, no auto-release. (3) `twilio_webhooks` thread lookup sorts by `last_message_at` desc so the reply lands in the freshest (lead) thread. (4) Website "New Lead" alerts carry `conversation_id` (alert opens the lead thread); legacy `/demo-requests/{id}/claim` returns `{status:"routed", conversation_id}` instead of minting a duplicate contact for source-routed leads. Regression: `backend/tests/vanishing_leads_regression.py` (12/12). Needs backend Deploy + `eas update`.
- **Lead Threads Merge + Release Warning + Flow Analytics (June 2026) - DONE, tested (`backend/tests/returning_lead_merge_e2e.py` 40/40; regressions vanishing 12/12, lead_flows 22/22, ai_routing 41/41; UI verified on preview)**: (1) MERGE: `process_inbound_lead` for a `returning_owner` lead REUSES the owner's freshest open conversation with that contact (`lead_merged: True`, `lead_count` +1, `lead_created_at` = now, release/alert timers re-armed, `routing_resolved: False`, thread's `created_at`/`ai_mode`/`rep_phone` kept; after-hours + Jessi flips ai_mode auto_reply) and inserts a system message `is_lead_marker: True` ("New Website lead from this customer · <vehicle>"); thread renders `sender:'system'` as a centered chip (gold when lead marker) instead of a contact bubble (`system-event-row`). LEAD CLOCK RULE: any "since the lead landed" math must use `lead_created_at or created_at` (`lead_queue._window/_lead_query/_build_items`, `_owner_replied_elsewhere`, `_first_human_replies` + `_message_stats` via `lead_intake.lead_floors()/lead_floor_stages()` which drop pre-lead messages on merged threads, `timeline_for_conversation`, messages.py `lead_received_at` x2, `/leads/awaiting`). (2) RELEASE WARNING: `process_returning_lead_escalations` (every minute) pushes + notifies (`lead_releasing_soon`, once via `owner_warned`) the owner `RELEASE_WARNING_MINUTES`=5 before `release_at`; `POST /api/leads/queue/{user_id}/keep/{conv_id}` ("I've got this", owner or manager, 403 otherwise, idempotent `already`) sets `routing_resolved/kept_at/kept_by`, clears timers, dismisses the warning, logs a system message; escalation job skips `routing_resolved` convs. Timeline API gained `returning{is_returning, merged_thread, lead_count, release_at, resolved, kept_at, released_at, release_reason}` and its `iso()` now emits tz-aware UTC strings (was naive -> JS parsed as local). `components/LeadCallTimeline.tsx`: returning title/tone, T-minus banner (`lead-release-warning`, red <=5 min) + gold `lead-keep-btn`, `lead-kept-note`. (3) FLOW ANALYTICS: `services/lead_flows.flow_stats(db, flow_ids, days)` (leads excl. is_test, claimed %, median speed-to-claim from claimed_at-lead_created_at, reply %, median first human reply, ladder_runs/no_answer % from lead_call_jobs status exhausted) attached as `stats` on `GET /lead-flows` (list), `GET /lead-flows/{id}` and new `GET /lead-flows/{id}/stats?days=` (managers only); intake stamps `flow_id` on conversations + inbound_leads. `components/admin/LeadFlowSummary.tsx` `FlowStatsStrip` on library cards (`lead-flow-stats-{id}`) and editor (`lead-flow-stats`). - **PROD image 404s root cause + fix (June 2026) - DONE, verified against prod `/api/images/_health`**: production logs showed `object storage returned 404` for profile photos / store logos that DO exist. Cause: production runs with a DIFFERENT `EMERGENT_LLM_KEY` (`sk-emergent-80…`) than preview (`sk-emergent-f0…`) and the Emergent object-storage namespace is tied to the key used at `/init`, so every image uploaded under the f0 key (all historical uploads, 100+ profile objects) is invisible to prod. FIX: `utils/image_storage.py` now inits storage with `OBJECT_STORAGE_KEY` (falls back to `EMERGENT_LLM_KEY`); `backend/.env` pins `OBJECT_STORAGE_KEY` to the f0 key; `/api/images/_health` reports `key_source`. After redeploy, prod `/api/images/_health` must show `key_source: OBJECT_STORAGE_KEY`, `key_hint: sk-emergent-f0…`, `sample_status: 200`. If prod env vars are managed separately in the deploy settings, OBJECT_STORAGE_KEY must be added there too. Also: public pages now swap a missing image for an initials bubble (`IMG_FALLBACK` onerror) so a 404 never shows a broken-image icon. Deployment agent run: no blockers (its CORS/.gitignore warnings were false positives: prod domains are in CORS_ORIGINS, /app/.gitignore exists).

- **AI Auto-Reply Suppression Bug FIXED (June 2026, this session — PROD RCA)**: User reported AI auto-replies not sending in PRODUCTION despite "Jessi is handling this". Deployer debug confirmed: NOTHING was queued (no ai_reply_queue docs), "You're Needed" escalation fired instead. ROOT CAUSE (reproduced in preview): triple suppression in twilio_webhooks.py + ai_reply.py — (1) `conv_ai_off` treated stale `ai_enabled: False` as AI-off even when ai_mode='auto_reply' explicit; (2) broken `enrollment_ai_queued` any() comprehension forced campaign=None and read stale enrollment ai_assist_mode, wrongly skipping the conversation-level AI block; (3) same stale-flag bug in process_ai_reply_queue's rep-override check cancelled queued items with cancel_reason='ai_disabled_by_rep'. FIXES: explicit active ai_mode (auto_reply/draft_only/auto_with_approval) now ALWAYS wins over stale ai_enabled=False in all 3 places; conversation-level 'auto_reply' also overrides campaign mode in the enrollment loop (no approval gate); enrollment_ai_queued now tracked from actually-fired modes; added diagnostic logs ("No AI queued for conv...", "AI suppressed...", "Mode off — not queuing"). Verified e2e in preview via simulated Twilio webhook (POST /api/webhooks/twilio/incoming, no signature check): before fix = 0 queue docs (bug reproduced); after fix = queued auto_reply → scheduler sent REAL Twilio SMS (mocked:False) → ai message logged. You're-Needed escalation still fires in parallel (unchanged). Also HARDENED the Home master AI switch (was a suspect, not the cause): confirm dialog before pausing, red "AI PAUSED" pill (was subtle gray "AI OFF"), amber "All AI is paused (Home switch)" banner in thread with Resume AI button, and backend now sends notification+push when a customer replies while master-paused (was a silent drop in queue_ai_reply). NOTE: FIX IS IN PREVIEW ONLY — needs production Deploy to take effect. Test data cleaned up, forest's default_campaign_id restored.

- **Broadcast MMS Failure Fix (June 2026, this session — USER-REPORTED, PRODUCTION)**: "Blast to Jessica and Forest didn't go out." Deployer RCA: Twilio error 21620 — the composer pushed the RAW LOCAL iOS file:// path into media_urls (upload code was a stubbed TODO: `uploadedUrls.push(img.uri)`), Twilio can't fetch device paths; both sends attempted+rejected, docs marked pending_user_action with NO error stored, broadcast stuck status='sending' forever. FIXES: (1) broadcast/new.tsx now actually uploads photos to POST /api/images/upload (public, auth-exempt, Twilio-fetchable) and uses original_url; upload failure aborts with a clear alert. (2) broadcast.py send endpoint sanitizes media_urls (keeps http(s), prefixes /api/ paths with PUBLIC_FACING_URL/APP_URL, DROPS file:///data: junk) so old drafts self-heal to text-only. (3) scheduler.py: Twilio failures now persist status='failed' + error text on the pending doc, skip fake message/event logging; new _update_broadcast_progress() increments broadcasts.sent_count/failed_count and finalizes status sent/failed + completed_at when queue drains; broadcast sends marked 'sent' (was mislabeled pending_user_action since delivery_mode 'auto'≠'automated'). E2E-verified in preview with unroutable +19999999999 (no real SMS): file:// dropped, quiet-hours gate confirmed (deferred at UTC midnight → tz temporarily Auckland to test), Twilio 21211 → doc failed w/ error, broadcast finalized 'failed', all test data cleaned + tz restored. ROOT CAUSE: `/users/{id}/sold-performance` anchored 'current month' to SERVER UTC (rolled to Sept 1 while user's phone was still Aug 31); sales-list uses client-local date → mismatch. FIX: sold-performance + sold-monthly-summary accept optional `month`/`year` params anchoring the series to the CLIENT's local date; home.tsx (2 call sites) + sales-list.tsx now pass local month/year. Verified via curl: unanchored=Sep 0, anchored Aug=correct counts. RULE: any 'this month/this week' server counting must accept a client-local anchor. NOTE: needs BOTH backend redeploy AND eas update to fully fix production.

ROOT CAUSE: ~20 copy handlers were WEB-ONLY (`navigator.clipboard`/`execCommand` gated behind Platform.OS==='web') — on the native iOS/Android app they copied NOTHING but still showed "Copied!". RULE FOR FUTURE AGENTS: NEVER write `navigator.clipboard` directly — always `import { copyToClipboard } from 'utils/clipboard'`. Tested via testing_agent iteration_288: 8/9 flows verified with real clipboard READ-BACK on web (correct URLs). ALSO: removed 597 lines of DEAD CODE in more.tsx (3 unreachable share modals — review/showcase/birthday — plus their handlers/states/styles/match-modal; no trigger ever opened them; Hub verified renders after); fixed latent crash: Delete Account used showAlert without import; added testID+dataSet to share-copy-link/share-via-link/error-reports-copy/header-qr-btn/my-profile share chips. ## iOS Avatar Rendering Fix (June 2026)
- Root cause: several screens used React Native core `Image` for REMOTE photo avatars. Renders on web, unreliable on iOS with newArchEnabled=true. Working iOS avatars (inbox/thread/more) all use `expo-image`.
- Switched to `expo-image` (contentFit + memory-disk cache): app/sales-list.tsx, app/l/[username].tsx, app/p/store/[slug].tsx.
- Left on core Image intentionally: local require() logo assets (imos/_components.tsx), import-guide screenshots, white-label (Image import unused).
- DELIVERY: frontend/JS change — requires `eas update --branch production` (+ app reopen). Backend deploy alone does NOT update the installed app's JS.

## Relationship Intelligence — Scope C (June 2026)
Built the "IMOS remembers the person" layer. 5 items + score:
1. Relationship Profile at top of contact card — ALREADY existed (IntelBriefingCard @ contact/[id].tsx line ~2539); verified in place.
2. People to Talk To Today feed — NEW screen app/people-today.tsx + endpoint GET /api/home/people-to-engage/{user_id}?limit (widened get_my_3 via top_n param). Home "See all →" link added to the "Your 3 for Today" header.
3. Relationship Health Score + Book of Business rollup — NEW router routers/relationship_health.py:
   - GET /relationship-health/{user_id}/summary  (bucket rollup)
   - GET /relationship-health/{user_id}/contacts?bucket=  (drill-down, worst-first)
   - GET /relationship-health/{user_id}/contact/{contact_id}  (badge, scoped by user_id)
   Buckets: opportunity(🔥 recent engagement/sold<30d) / at_risk(🔴 90d+) / cooling(🟡 45-90d) / advocate(💙 review or referrer) / connected(🟢 <45d).
   Last-touch derived from contact_events (real event types), NOT the never-written last_contacted_at. Advocate = review events + advocate/referral tags + name appears as a referrer (referred_by_name reverse lookup). 30s TTL cache.
   Frontend: app/book-of-business.tsx, components/home/BookOfBusinessCard.tsx (home), components/contact/HealthBadge.tsx (contact card).
4. Human-readable timeline — Feed tab already renders human event titles/descriptions; no change needed.
5. Touch tagging (Transactional/Promotional/Relationship) — GET /api/home/touch-mix/{user_id}?days heuristic classifier; surfaced as "X% real relationship touches this week" line in BookOfBusinessCard.

- health_one now filters db.contacts by user_id (was cross-tenant readable).

Verified: tests/test_relationship_health_290_auth.py 10/10 PASS (401 unauth, 403 non-owner). Functional contracts verified via curl with token.

DELIVERY: backend deploy + `eas update --branch production`. Frontend not visually testable in headless preview (Expo web renders blank in automation) — confirm on device.

## After-Hours Rule + TCPA window + Test Lead + Attempt Timeline (June 2026) - DONE, tested (iteration_293: 26/26 new pytest + 20/20 ladder + 28/28 AI routing + frontend flows)
- USER DECISIONS: Jessi answers ANY inbound customer text 24/7 (consumer-initiated, no consent tiering); only BUSINESS-INITIATED contact (intake text, rep call ladder) respects a texting window in the CUSTOMER's local time; anything outside the window releases next morning STAGGERED 1 lead/min per store. Jessi answers lead replies whenever the source's "Enable AI Auto-Reply" toggle is on (hours or not) OR the store is closed under the after-hours rule.
- services/lead_timing.py: build_contact_plan(db, source, store, phone) -> plan {customer_tz (phonenumbers area code, ambiguous -> store tz), window{start,end,inside,opens_at}, store{configured,open,opens_at}, intake_at/intake_deferred/intake_reason, ladder_at/ladder_deferred/ladder_reasons ['texting_window'|'store_closed'], jessi_on}. Stagger via db.lead_release_slots {key scope:ISOminute, count}. Store hours = stores.business_hours + timezone (Store Profile screen); opening buffer +5 min.
- Lead Source workflow fields: after_hours_mode 'text_and_ai'|'ring_anyway' (default text_and_ai), text_window_start/end HH:MM (default 09:00-20:00 = strict-state preset; federal preset 08:00-21:00; PUT validates HH:MM -> 400). GET /workflow also returns store_hours {timezone, configured, open_now, opens_at, hours}.
- lead_intake.process_inbound_lead: computes plan, stores conversation.routing_plan, after_hours_lead, ai_mode 'auto_reply'+ai_enabled when plan.jessi_on (else legacy inert 'assist'), sms_consent (from demo form sms_opt_in -> also contact.sms_consent*), is_test + 'Test Lead' tag. Legacy AI first message (inbound_leads.status) is now 'skipped' when the source has an intake_text (was double-texting). _fire_intake_workflow: intake from ASSIGNED rep's Twilio number first (else on-shift pool), sets conversation.rep_phone so the customer's reply routes into the lead thread; deferred intake -> db.lead_deferred_actions (scheduler process_lead_deferred_actions every 30s; cancelled 'rep_replied' if a human already texted); rep push body gains "After hours: Jessi is replying, calls ring at 9:05 AM". Ladder: start_call_workflow(not_before, deferred_reasons) -> job.deferred/deferred_until; process_lead_call_jobs on a deferred first rung skips if conversation claimed (any source) or a rep already texted (status 'handled', handled_reason rep_replied); on fire sets deferred False/was_deferred/released_at; answer prompt says "Overnight lead".
- twilio_webhooks.py: when no contact in the rep namespace, reuse the internet-lead contact from the conversation matched by rep_phone+contact_phone (no more duplicate 'Lead (xxxx)'); verified by stubbed probe (tests/manual_lead_reply_probe.py) + ai_routing_matrix 28/28.
- Timeline: webhooks record answered_at (/answer CallSid), passed (Digits!=1), late; record_call_status keeps keypress outcomes. GET /api/lead-sources/call-timeline/{conversation_id} (manager, or owner/assignee/claimer/ladder member; else 403) -> {plan, intake{sent_at,scheduled_for}, jessi_on, sms_consent, claimed_*, job{status, deferred, was_deferred, deferred_until, attempts, calls[{attempt,name,outcome claimed|passed|late|answered|no_answer|ringing|no_phone|failed}], time_to_claim_seconds (from first ring for deferred jobs), exhausted_at}}.
- POST /api/lead-sources/{id}/test-lead {phone, first_name, last_name, include_ladder} (manager) runs the REAL pipeline (intake SMS, pushes, ladder) tagged Test Lead; returns plan + flags. Frontend components/admin/LeadTimingControls.tsx: AfterHoursRule (mode toggle, store-hours line w/ Edit -> /settings/store-profile, TCPA presets + custom inputs) and TestLeadCard (phone prefilled with admin's cell, name, ladder checkbox, ARM-THEN-TAP confirm "Tap again to text ••••1234 and ring reps", result lines, Open thread). components/LeadCallTimeline.tsx on internet-lead threads (thread/[id].tsx isInternetLead) with header state (Claimed by X in m:ss / Ringing attempt n of N / rings at 9:05 AM / skipped / exhausted) + expandable rows + consent line.
- Also converted raw data-testid -> testID+dataSet on admin/lead-sources/[id].tsx and auth/login.tsx (login-email-input/login-password-input now in DOM). serialize_lead_source now falls back webhook_url to /api/lead-sources/inbound/{id}.
- Test helpers: tests/test_lead_after_hours_293.py (run with -p no:randomly), tests/cleanup_test_leads.py (purges is_test data + slots), tests/manual_*_probe.py. SAFETY unchanged: 500-555 numbers only, Activation Tester only on preview ladders.
- SHIP: backend Deploy (all rules) + `eas update` (Lead Source screen + thread timeline). Production stores need business_hours + timezone set in Store Profile for the after-hours rule to engage (no hours = always "open").

## Relationship Intel now reads texts + calls (June 2026) - DONE, verified e2e in preview (test_contact_intel.py 13/13)
- USER: "when updating the relationship intel on opening a contact, review the text conversation in the inbox and contextually update info just like a voice note or phone call". AUDIT FOUND: briefing queried messages by contact_id+created_at but inbox texts are stored by conversation_id+timestamp (Bud: 19 texts, briefing saw 1); new texts did not flag stale; personal_details (from voice notes) were not in the prompt; call transcripts not included; no extraction from texts/calls.
- FIX (backend only, routers/contact_intel.py + services/voice_intel.py): (1) `_message_match` = messages by contact_id OR any conversation of the contact, sorted by timestamp||created_at (aggregation), labeled "← Customer" / "→ You" / "→ Jessi (AI assistant)" / "· Call"; drafts + system events excluded. (2) Prompt adds KNOWN PERSONAL DETAILS (contact.personal_details + vehicle/occupation/employer/city) and RECORDED PHONE CALLS (call_logs transcript[:600] + ai_summary). (3) `_latest_activity` covers conv messages + call_logs; ignores event_type intelligence_extracted (AI's own event, else self-perpetuating stale). (4) `process_conversation_intelligence(user_id, contact_id)` in voice_intel.py: runs `extract_personal_details(text, source='text'|'call')` (EXTRACTION_PROMPT now has __SOURCE__ placeholder; SOURCE_DESCRIPTIONS) over NEW customer texts (>=15 chars) + new call transcripts since contacts.conv_intel_cursor.at, merges via merge_personal_details (newest wins), logs contact_event "Personal Details Updated / AI updated from texts: ...", advances cursor. Kicked off as asyncio task in generate_contact_intel in PARALLEL with the briefing (~10s total, briefing dominates); response gains `details_updated` list; data_points gains `calls`. Briefing output wrapped in no_em_dash(). No voice-note-style auto follow-up task from texts (avoids task spam).
- Verified: seeded inbound text on Bud -> GET stale:true -> POST regen: messages 20 (was 1), briefing cited job change + Bear Lake + brother Tom, personal_details.employer -> Fidelity, event logged, cursor set; GET stale:false; 2nd regen details_updated [] (no re-extraction). Test data restored.
- SHIP: backend Deploy only.

## Voicemail Follow-Up + Lead Handoff Note (June 2026) - DONE (backend verified via scripts + webhook curl; UI screenshots for release sheet/handoff card)
- **Detection (NO AMD)**: Twilio `<Dial>` AMD is synchronous only (would delay/clip every answered call), so we use (1) `<Dial action=/api/webhooks/twilio/call-bridge-result>` -> DialCallStatus busy/no-answer/failed/canceled or completed<8s => immediate miss; completed => (2) the transcript we already make: services/call_followup.detect_outcome = VOICEMAIL_HINTS heuristic -> gpt-5.2 one-word classify -> duration fallback (>=60s connected). 240s fallback timer if no recording callback. pending_calls gains dial_status/dial_duration_s/customer_outcome/outcome_source/outcome_applied (idempotent claim). call_logs.outcome set; ai_summary prefixed "[Voicemail]"/"[No answer]"; appointment extraction skipped for voicemail. Task-you-called-from now completes ONLY on connected (was: any recording).
- **Cadence** (Forest): miss #1 -> +30 min (after 8 PM -> next business 10 AM); #2 -> +3h same day (after 7 PM -> next morning); #3 -> next business morning 10 AM + "just tried you" text tip; #4 -> +2 days 10 AM; #5 -> single final "{n} tries, no connection: text or park" task (auto_kind call_retry_final); then stop. Sundays skipped. Streak resets after 7 days idle or on any connected call (closes open call_retry tasks). State on contacts.call_retry {attempts,last_at,last_outcome,next_due,task_id}. Retry task reuse order: open call_retry task -> the task the rep called from (rescheduled + tagged auto_kind call_retry) -> new task (type call, source voicemail_followup, title "Try {First} again"). Push "{First} went to voicemail / didn't answer" -> /contact/{id}?taskId=; notification type call_retry_scheduled; contact_event call_voicemail|call_no_answer|call_busy.
- **Manual path**: POST /api/calls/{uid}/outcome {contact_id, outcome connected|voicemail|no_answer|busy, duration, conversation_id?, task_id?} (services.call_followup.record_manual_outcome). "/api/calls/" added to PROTECTED_PREFIXES (401 without token). call-screen.tsx native "Log Call" modal gained "How did it go?" chips (call-outcome-*) + cadence hint; Talked completes the task; misses schedule the retry and show "Follow-up set" alert.
- **Lead Handoff Note**: POST /leads/queue/{uid}/release/{conv} body {note} (<=200 chars) -> conversations.handoff_note {text,by,by_name,at,kind release|reassign}, system message "... · Note for the next rep: "..."", "Up for grabs" push + notification body = `Forest: "note"`. Reassign note also persisted as handoff_note. `_build_items` returns handoff_note; LeadsQueuePanel shows amber "↳ Forest: ..." block (lead-handoff-{id}); Release now opens a sheet (lead-release-sheet / lead-release-note / lead-release-confirm "Release with note|without note" / lead-release-cancel); reassign sheet has an optional note field (lead-reassign-note).
- Tests: /tmp scripts (engine cadence, idempotent webhook replay, manual endpoint 401/400/200), test_lead_queue_294 12/12, ai_routing_matrix 28/28. NOTE: engine test sent 2 real pushes to Forest ("Bud went to voicemail") - explained to user.
- SHIP: backend Deploy + eas update.

## Proof: per-source spend editor + returning-customer clock fix (June 2026) - DONE (probe test 9/9, UI round-trip verified)
- USER (prod screenshot): could not edit cost per source (prompt vanished once priced; Lead Source Config field hidden behind Edit) and saw "first touch -6792999s" + "67.7 touches avg".
- ROOT CAUSE of the bad numbers: services/lead_clocks.clocks_for_leads matched messages by conversation_id with NO time floor, so a returning customer routed into an existing thread counted months of old texts/calls (negative first-touch, inflated touch counts). FIX: events (messages, calls, call_logs, press-1 bridges) are now filtered PER LEAD to >= received_at - 5 min in Python (mixed str/datetime timestamps make a Mongo-side floor unsafe). compute_proof also drops negative touch deltas. Test: tests/test_clock_returning_customer.py (seeds + cleans; 9/9 PASS).
- UI (ProofPanel): every source row now has a gold pill for managers: "Add spend" (no cost) or "$1,500/mo" (pencil) -> inline SpendEditor ($ input "/mo", Save, live math line "$X over N days ÷ leads = $/lead · ÷ sold = $/sale"); PATCH /lead-sources/{id} monthly_cost (0 clears). Old SourceCostPrompt (Skip/AsyncStorage) REMOVED - single way to price a source. Footnote rewritten. testids spend-edit-{id}, spend-input-{id}, spend-save-{id}, spend-math-{id}, spend-editor-{id}. Sources with 0 leads in the window are not listed (price them when they start producing).

## Metro stale-bundle ROOT CAUSE FIXED (June 2026)
- frontend/watch-patch.js (loaded via NODE_OPTIONS in supervisor) replaced fs.watch with polling on the watched path's OWN mtime. A directory's mtime only changes on add/remove, so EDITS to existing files were never seen -> stale bundles / lint loops (15+ recurrences). Patch now snapshots directory entries (name -> mtime) for dirs under /app/frontend outside node_modules/.git/.metro-cache/.expo and emits change/rename per file every 2s. Verified: sed edit to lead-connect.tsx appeared in the served bundle within 8s without restart. New route files still need one `supervisorctl restart frontend` (expo-router route map), edits do not.
