## Spend math fix: only months actually paying count (June 2026) - DONE (tests 7/7 + 12/12, UI verified)
- USER (prod): $1,000/mo Website, 2 sold, 90-day view showed $3,000 spent / $1,500 per sale. Cause: monthly cost was prorated across the whole window even though the source only started paying this month.
- FIX compute_proof.months_charged(started): full window (days/30) when the source was paying before the window; otherwise min(days/30, max(1, ceil(overlap_days/30))) -> $1,000/mo started this month = $1,000 in 30/90/365-day views, 7-day view still prorates ($233). started = lead_sources.spend_started_at (ISO, user override via PATCH /lead-sources) else the source's first-ever real lead. Same for campaigns (campaign_costs.spend_started_at via PUT /leads/campaign-costs, default first lead of that campaign). Rows now return months_charged + spend_started_at.
- UI: SpendEditor has PAYING SINCE chips: "Since first lead (Aug 27)" (default when no override) / This month / Last month / 2 months ago / Longer (13 months back); math preview uses the same formula. Source and campaign rows show the SpendMath line "$1,000/mo x 1 mo paying in this window = $1,000 ÷ 2 sold = $500 / sale · paying since Sep 2026". Footnote rewritten with the $1,000 example.
- Tests: tests/test_spend_months_charged.py, tests/test_ad_level_proof.py (expectations updated for 1-month charge).

## Dialer keypad enlarged (June 2026) - DONE (375x812 web check: 84pt buttons, keypad fixed while typing, search view unaffected)
- app/(tabs)/dialer.tsx: button size now measured from the real keypad column height (onLayout -> btnSize(padH)) instead of a guessed CHROME_H constant; cap raised 80 -> 84, width cap (PAD_SIDE-64-28)/3, floor 52. Reserved column: status line 24 + number 52 + match strip 44+6 + paddings; ROW_GAP 12. Digits/icons scale with the button.
- Contact matches while dialing became a single fixed-height horizontal chip strip (up to 4 chips + "N more" which drops the digits into the search box to show the full list) instead of a 90pt two-row list, which is where the extra height came from.
- Also fixed a pre-existing hooks-order violation: the "Access Pending" early return sat above useMemo/useEffect; moved below all hooks.
- NOTE: dialer.tsx uses data-testid props (not dataSet), so they do not reach the web DOM; use text selectors when screenshot-testing it.

## Hub -> "Apps" iPhone-style home screen (June 2026) - DONE (web verified: folders open, jiggle edit mode, drag reorder, move sheet, persistence)
- Tab renamed Hub -> Apps (icon apps). app/(tabs)/more.tsx keeps ALL section-building/role-gating logic and the search bar, profile card, rep preview, legal footer; the task grid + pinned chips + collapsible section lists + Admin block were replaced by <AppHome/>.
- Catalog: every section item -> HubApp {id: slug(title), folder: section id} with FOLDER_FOR remap (account_mgmt+internal_ops -> "admin", setup_manage -> "setup"); My Brand _brandItems included; the 6 task-grid quick actions become loose apps by default (hubDefaultLoose).
- components/hub/: layout.ts (HubLayout {v:1, home: ["f:<folder>"|"a:<app>"], folders:{id:{title,items}}}, reconcile() drops hidden apps / adds new apps to their default folder / removes empty folders; loadLayout (AsyncStorage hub_layout_v1:<uid> vs users.hub_layout by updated_at), saveLayout (debounced 400ms, PATCH /users/{id} {hub_layout}), clearLayout). Tiles.tsx (AppTile glassy dark tinted tile, FolderTile 3x3 mini icons, Badge, Jiggle via reanimated withRepeat rotate). SortableGrid.tsx (absolute slots + LinearTransition; RNGH Pan enabled only in edit mode: onBegin lifts a ghost overlay, onUpdate computes target slot -> onReorder, onFinalize springs to slot; Pressable handles tap / long-press-to-edit; onDragging disables the outer ScrollView). FolderModal.tsx (blur backdrop, ZoomIn card, Edit/Done, rename input, inner SortableGrid, tap-in-edit -> MoveSheet). MoveSheet.tsx (Home / any folder / New folder). AppHome.tsx orchestrates (ARRANGE header with Reset + Done, folder rename/remove sheet).
- Backend: server.py PATCH /api/users/{id} allows hub_layout.
- **Apps Phase 2 gestures (June 2026) - DONE, verified on web preview at 390px**: (1) Hold an app over another app (dwell 600ms, centre zone = 32% of tile) -> new folder (title from the target's default section if that folder does not exist yet, else "New Folder", auto-suffixed -2); over a folder -> files it (folder tile goes gold + scales while hovered). Lingering off-centre shifts tiles out of the way like iOS (SETTLE_MS 170 then decide). (2) Drag out of a folder: FolderModal grid passes onDragOutside (outsideMargin 48px past the grid); AppHome.dragOut moves the app home, closes the folder, gold Landing glow on the tile (~1.7s); empty folders are deleted, one-app folders remain. (3) RECENT row (device-local AsyncStorage hub_recent:<uid>, last 3 launched incl. launches from inside folders, hidden while arranging). Verified: app-on-app folder, app-onto-folder, drag-out, recent row, Reset, layout persisted after reload. Frontend-only -> `eas update`.
- **Folder badge tap + Recent sync (June 2026) - DONE, verified on preview**: (1) Red badge on a folder tile is now a Pressable (Tiles.tsx Badge onPress, testid tile-badge-btn, hitSlop 10, stopPropagation so the folder does not open; disabled while arranging/dragging). AppHome.badgeTap: exactly one badged app in the folder -> launch it directly (counts as recent); several -> open the folder. Verified: Leads folder badge -> /leads directly. (2) Recent row synced to the account: layout.ts RecentEntry {id, at ISO}, mergeRecent (union, newest per id, max 6 stored / 3 shown), local cache AsyncStorage hub_recent_v2:<uid>, remote via GET /api/users/{id}.hub_recent on focus (merge + cache) and debounced PATCH /api/users/{id} {hub_recent} on launch. Backend server.py PATCH allows hub_recent with shape validation (list of {id:str, at:str}, max 12 -> 400 otherwise). Verified: fresh browser (empty localStorage) shows the account's recent row; bad payloads 400. SHIP: backend Deploy + `eas update`.
- **No-bounce motion + back-into-folder (June 2026, USER FEEDBACK) - DONE, verified on web**: All springs removed from the Apps grid (SortableGrid lift/drop/reflow -> withTiming / LinearTransition.duration(220) with Easing.out(cubic); Landing glow no longer scales; FolderModal open = ZoomIn.duration(180) eased, no springify). Arrange-mode wiggle (Jiggle) kept. Back navigation: AppHome.open() stores the folder id in returnFolder ref before launching; useFocusEffect reopens that folder (restore prop = quick 80ms fade instead of zoom) when the Apps tab regains focus; apps launched from the home grid / recent row / badge do not reopen anything. Verified: Leads folder -> Internet Leads -> back lands inside Leads; home app round trip stays closed.
- **Tab renamed Apps -> Tools + Folder pages (June 2026) - DONE, verified on web**: (1) User picked "Tools" over Apps/Hub/Settings/More: (tabs)/_layout title 'Tools' + icon 'grid'; AppHome header TOOLS; all hint copy says "tool" (AppHome, FolderModal, MoveSheet counts, remove-folder note). (2) FolderModal REWRITTEN with iPhone-style pages: PAGE_SIZE 8 (4x2), horizontal pagingEnabled ScrollView (testid folder-pager, pages folder-page-N, grids folder-grid / folder-grid-N), gold dot indicator (folder-dot-N tappable), scroll disabled while arranging. Per-page SortableGrid; reorderPage() splices the page back into the full id list. Cross-page move in arrange mode = "edge hop": SortableGrid new props onEdgeHold(key, dir) fired after the tile hangs >=24px off the left/right grid edge for 450ms (cancelDrag ends the drag, no drop anim), outsideAxis 'vertical' on paged folders so left/right pulls never trigger drag-out (up/down still does). hop(): app becomes first tool of next page / last of previous (displaced tool swaps pages, like iOS), pager scrolls, Landing glow. initialPage prop: AppHome returnFolder now {id, page} so back lands on the same page. Verified on Admin (4 pages): dots nav, hop right (Account Health -> page 2 slot 1, Activity Feed swapped back), open on page 2 -> back reopens page 2.

- **Tools tab header condensed (June 2026, USER REQUEST "condense the space at the top, put search at the top") - DONE, verified**: more.tsx: removed the tall cover-photo profile card + two big My Profile / My VA buttons + full-width View as Rep row. New order: search bar with the notification bell beside it (hubTopRow / hubBell), then a compact profile strip (44px avatar, name, gold title or email, small Profile + My VA pills; testids profile-card, edit-profile-hint-btn, virtual-assistant-btn kept), then a tiny right-aligned "View as Rep" link (admins), then TOOLS. Grid now starts ~2.5x higher on screen. Removed unused LinearGradient / resolvePhotoUrl imports. Frontend only -> `eas update`.
- **Multi-photo replies + delivery receipts (June 2026) - DONE, verified**: Inventory now has a photo gallery per vehicle (`photos: [{full_path, thumb_url}]`, MAX_PHOTOS 6, legacy single cover folded in by _gallery(); cover = photos[0] mirrored to photo_url/photo_full_path). Endpoints: POST /inventory/{u}/{item}/photo appends (400 at 6), DELETE /inventory/{u}/{item}/photo/{index}, PUT /inventory/{u}/{item}/photo/{index}/cover. GET list returns photos. Frontend: components/inventory/PhotoGallerySheet.tsx (grid, COVER / SENT #2 / SENT #3 / EXTRA labels, tap = make cover, X = delete, Add tile), inventory.tsx tile shows a photo-count badge; first tap with no photos still adds directly. Also fixed a cold-start race: inventory.tsx redirected to login before the session hydrated (now waits for authStore.isLoading). Jessi: ai_reply._search_inventory_context -> _photos(it) (gallery, legacy cover, then feed images/primary_image; all /api/images URLs get ?format=jpeg); "specific" when exactly one candidate is named in the message (name/model/trim/stock #) -> up to 3 photos of that vehicle; comparisons / broad asks -> one cover per vehicle, up to 3; falls back to one-per-vehicle when the named car has no photos. Verified: "Do you still have the Tacoma TRD?" -> 3 photos; "Tacoma or the F-150?" -> 1 cover each with photos. Delivery receipts: MessageBubble shows green "Delivered" (checkmark-done) when status == 'delivered' (set by /webhooks/twilio/status, which now fires because send_sms passes status_callback); thread poll now re-renders on status changes, not just new messages. Verified with a simulated delivered callback. SHIP: backend Deploy + `eas update`.
- **Jessi MMS never delivered (June 2026, USER-REPORTED on PRODUCTION: Gladiator photo reply showed in the app but never reached the phone) - ROOT CAUSE + FIX, verified on preview**: Not quiet hours. Inventory photos are stored as WebP (image_storage.upload_image -> {id}.webp, served image/webp at /api/images/...). Twilio MMS only accepts jpeg/png/gif (+heic/tiff/bmp); WebP -> error 12300 Invalid Content-Type, message fails at the carrier AFTER the API accepted it. ai_reply._search_inventory_context built the media URL without ?format=jpeg (every other MMS path already used it), and process_ai_reply_queue marked the item SENT + wrote the message as "sent" without checking result.success, and send_sms never passed status_callback nor returned "sid" (ai_reply read result["sid"] -> None, so status callbacks could not even match AI messages). Fixes: (1) twilio_service.mms_safe_media() appends ?format=jpeg to every /api/images/ URL lacking format= (central safety net for ALL MMS), send_sms passes status_callback=<PUBLIC_FACING_URL>/api/webhooks/twilio/status and returns both message_sid and sid. (2) ai_reply inventory URLs explicitly ?format=jpeg. (3) process_ai_reply_queue: on send failure with media -> retry text-only (media_dropped flag); still failing -> queue FAILED, message stored status "failed" + error_message, push to rep via _notify_send_failure ("Jessi's text to X did not send"). (4) twilio_webhooks /status: on failed/undelivered -> _handle_failed_outbound: if has_media and ErrorCode in MEDIA_ERROR_CODES (11200,12300,12400,21620,21623,30008) resend text-only from the same number (new message retry_of=<sid>, media_dropped=True) and push the rep; any other failure -> push "did not deliver" (failure_handled flag prevents repeats). (5) messages API returns error_message + media_dropped; MessageBubble shows red "Not delivered · reason" and orange "Sent without the photo (carrier rejected it)" badges. Verified: /api/images/<inv>.webp -> image/webp, ?format=jpeg -> image/jpeg; fake 12300 status callback -> text-only resend SM9f20..., original marked failed; thread shows both badges. SHIP: backend Deploy + `eas update`.
- **Thank Advocates one-step flow (June 2026) - DONE, verified**: Backend relationship_health.py: GET /advocates/{contact_id}/thank returns prefilled text + card drafts (rep first name, contact first name) and via_twilio flag; POST /advocates/{contact_id}/thank {mode: text|card, message?} -> card mode builds a thankyou card via congrats_cards.auto_create_card (custom_message = the note) and appends the short link; sends from the rep's Twilio/mvpline number (502 on Twilio failure), writes the conversation message, logs thank_you_text_sent / thankyou_card_sent (new EVENT_META + label), sets contacts.last_thanked_at, busts the book cache; no rep number -> via 'native' so the app opens Messages prefilled. Frontend: components/advocates/ThankSheet.tsx bottom sheet (text | card toggle, editable draft, Send), advocates.tsx CTA opens it, row shows "Thanked today" / "Thank again", header "N thanked this month", toast on success. Verified on preview: text + card sends via Twilio to the test advocate, both events on Activity with previews, UI state flips to Thanked today.
- **Book of Business: Advocate is a flag, not a bucket (June 2026, USER-REPORTED "7 advocates at top but 1 below") - DONE, verified**: Root cause: buckets are exclusive by priority, so an advocate 90+ days silent sat in At Risk; the tile counted warm advocates only (1) while the banner counted all (7). Fix (user chose option A): relationship_health.py BUCKET_META now 4 buckets (Opportunity / At Risk / Cooling / Connected, always sum to total); ADVOCATE_META separate; `_score` keeps is_advocate and prefixes reason "Advocate · 186 days silent"; summary returns advocate_meta; contacts?bucket=advocate kept as a legacy alias returning the advocates list (stale app builds); health_one returns advocate_meta. Frontend: book-of-business rows show a blue heart + blue reason for advocates in any group; the blue banner stays the one advocate entry point (-> /advocates); HealthBadge on the contact page shows a heart next to the health label; home BookOfBusinessCard shows "N advocates across every group - worth a thank you". Verified on preview: 4 tiles sum 62, banner 5, 2 advocate hearts inside At Risk. SHIP: backend Deploy + `eas update`.
- **Activity feed logs everything (June 2026, USER REQUEST "make sure everything is listed like new lead, text and calls") - DONE, verified**: Audit found the whole internet-lead pipeline wrote nothing to contact_events (the only source of the Activity tab). New central helper `utils/activity_log.py` (log_activity idempotent on `ref` = Twilio SID, MOCK_ refs ignored; on_lead_claimed moves store/org-owned rows for that contact to the claiming rep + logs lead_claimed; owner_for_contact / contact_for_conversation helpers; backfill_activity + run_backfill_once). Wired: lead_intake.process_inbound_lead -> new_lead / returning_lead (owner = assigned rep else store); _send_intake_sms + AI first message (process_queued_leads) -> auto_text_sent; messages /twilio-send -> event_type passed by app (personal_sms / mms_sent / vcf_sent / digital_card_sent...); /send-mms -> mms_sent; ai_reply approve -> ai_reply_sent; lead_call_engine._run_attempt -> lead_call_attempt on each rung rep's feed; lead_call_webhooks claim (press 1) -> lead_call_connected; claims (lead_sources claim + claim-and-call, lead_call_engine phone claim, chat_widget claim) -> on_lead_claimed; lead_queue reassign -> lead_reassigned, release -> lead_released. POST /contacts/{u}/{c}/events now folds a duplicate posted by older app screens into the auto-logged row (same contact+type within 90s with a ref) so quick-send flows do not double log. event_types.py: new labels + EVENT_META (icon/color/category). Master feed: description falls back to content_preview; new lead types classified as milestones. Startup migration `activity_backfill_v1` (db.migrations) backfills last 90 days: inbound_leads -> new_lead/returning_lead, messages -> sms_sent/ai_reply_sent/customer_reply/outbound_call (skips when any non-view event exists within ±120s), calls/call_logs -> outbound_call/inbound_call. Preview run: leads 5, texts 10, calls 0; second run all zeros. Frontend activity.tsx: milestone styling for new_lead/returning_lead/lead_claimed/lead_reassigned, one-line description preview under text/call/task rows and single cards. Verified end to end on preview: webhook lead -> claim -> twilio-send -> intake text -> release -> reassign all appear on Forest's Activity with previews. SHIP: backend Deploy (backfill runs itself once on prod startup) + `eas update`.

## Thread "No Phone" on Call Fix (June 2026) - DONE, verified in preview (USER-REPORTED from prod screenshot)
- Bug: opening a lead thread from the Home lead alert / push / inbox queue / anywhere that pushes plain `/thread/{id}` and tapping Call showed "No phone number for this contact." even though the intake text had just been sent.
- Root cause: thread/[id].tsx derived `contactPhone` ONLY from the `contact_phone` URL param; ~20 entry points open `/thread/{id}` without it.
- Fix: new `loadedContactPhone` state filled from GET /messages/conversation/{id}/info (contact_phone) or the contact record fallback (phone/phone_mobile); `contactPhone = param || loaded`. Header phone line, Call pill, composer call icon, share links all use it.
- Verified on preview: /thread/6a9bac3b62b000abb6c965ee opened with no params -> header shows (500) 555-0377, Call -> /call-screen?phone=+15005550377 (no alert).
- SHIP: frontend only -> `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Fix Call button No Phone on lead threads"`

## Jessi Off-Topic on Website Demo Leads FIX (June 2026) - DONE, verified in preview (USER-REPORTED from prod: "demo on that Mojave")
- ROOT CAUSE (4 parts): (1) AI prompts for lead threads never received the lead's inquiry (website form = software demo, company, business type, page, message); (2) clone prompt defaulted to "the dealership" and the rep's VA bio is automotive, plus relationship intel injected the contact's vehicle history + cross-thread old messages; (3) intent detector prompt hardcoded "automotive dealership CRM" ("schedule a demo visit" push); (4) inventory search fired on any lead.
- FIX: new services/lead_context.py (build_lead_inquiry / get_conversation_inquiry / inquiry_prompt_block / is_vehicle_inquiry). Inquiry stored on inbound_leads + conversations at intake; legacy lead threads derive it from attribution + inbound_lead and cache it (so the existing production thread is fixed on next reply). Block injected into auto-reply, follow-ups, Jessi suggests, first message, Home draft, intent detector. Non-vehicle inquiries skip inventory/shopping detection and vehicle history; intel reads only the lead thread's messages. "the dealership" -> "our team" default; intent prompt vertical-neutral. Lead Source > Response Workflow gained "What Are These Leads Asking About?" (inquiry_context, PUT/GET /lead-sources/{id}/workflow, admin/lead-sources/[id].tsx, testid inquiry-context-input).

## "Authentication required" on Import for Self-Signed-Up Users (June 2026) - FIXED, verified
- USER (Jess, prod screenshot): Import 625 Contacts -> "Could not preview: Authentication required" (401 from enforce_user_ownership middleware in server.py).
- ROOT CAUSE: store/authStore.ts `signup` stored a FAKE token `token_${user._id}` (backend /auth/signup never returned a JWT). verify_jwt_token rejects it -> every endpoint under PROTECTED_PREFIXES with an ObjectId in the path (contacts, tasks, messages, campaigns...) returned 401 "Authentication required"; unprotected screens worked so the app looked fine. The 401 interceptor only tried the cookie restore and then gave up -> stuck forever. Reproduced: fake token -> 401; real token -> 200.
- FIX: (1) /auth/signup now returns {**user, user, token: create_jwt_token(...)} + sets imonsocial_session/imonsocial_uid cookies like /login (password stripped, feature_permissions merged). (2) authStore.signup uses res.token (fallback: authAPI.login) and never fabricates a token. (3) services/api.ts 401 handler: if detail == "Authentication required" and the in-memory token is missing/not a 3-segment JWT/starts with token_ -> logout() (tabs layout redirects to /auth/login); a real JWT rejected twice -> logout too. IMMEDIATE WORKAROUND for affected users before the OTA: log out and log back in (login issues a real JWT).
- SHIP: backend Deploy + `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Fix sign-up sessions: real token, auto sign-out on dead session"`.

## Face ID Without Re-Entering Password (June 2026) - DONE, verified backend (7 cases) + screen renders
- USER: "If I'm already logged in, why do I have to re-enter my password to turn on Face ID? I don't remember it." OLD: biometrics stored email+PASSWORD in SecureStore and replayed /auth/login.
- NEW (token-based): POST /api/auth/refresh (Bearer or body token) -> fresh 30-day JWT + user + cookies via shared `_session_response` (also used by /auth/me). Accepts tokens expired <= 90 days (biometric unlock is the 2nd factor); rejects token_/mock_, bad signature, deactivated users, >90d ("It's been a while. Please log in with your password once."). Frontend: BiometricCredentials {email, token?, password?(legacy)}; Security toggle = Face ID confirm only, stores current store token; login.tsx/cs-login.tsx biometric login -> authStore.loginWithToken(token) (legacy password entries still honored); refreshBiometricToken() silently re-saves the newest token after every login/refresh so it never goes stale; post-login "Enable Face ID" stores the token, not the password. Security note copy updated ("Your password is never stored...").
- NOTE: preview JWT_SECRET is only 12 bytes (pyjwt warns <32). Production may differ; changing it logs everyone out, so left alone.
- SHIP: backend Deploy + `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Face ID setup without password"`.

## Announced Shop Calls (press 1 / ready gate) + Voicemail Fix (June 2026) - DONE, e2e ALL OK
- USER decisions: shops are really practice calls, reps will know it's AI -> announce it. Wording "your practice call from I'm On Social"; press 2 = bad time (back in ~2 h, no strike); Quick shops never auto-retry (admin taps Try again); announcement is the only mode (no blind switch).
- FLOW (services/scripts.py + routers/scripts.py): POST /twiml/{sid} for kind=mystery_shop returns `shop_gate_twiml`: <Gather input="dtmf speech" timeout=12 actionOnEmptyResult> + Polly.Joanna-Neural `shop_announcement` ("Hi Bud, this is your practice call from I'm On Social. Coming up: an inbound sales call. A customer is calling the store, so answer it exactly like a real phone-up. Press 1 or say ready when you're set. If now's a bad time, press 2 and we'll call back in a couple of hours." / outbound variant names the persona + vehicle: "You're calling Marcus back about the ... press 1 ... you'll hear it ring, and they'll pick up."). POST /gate/{sid} -> `gate_choice(Digits, SpeechResult)`: go (1 / ready-words) -> `shop_go_twiml` = Say "Here it comes." / "Here we go, it's ringing." + <Play> GET /api/scripts/roleplay/audio/ring.wav (US ringback 440+480 Hz, 5.8 s, generated once in memory; path is under /audio/ because /roleplay/{sid} GET on the auth router shadows /roleplay/ring.wav) + Connect ConversationRelay (inbound: no welcomeGreeting, customer waits for the rep's greeting; outbound: welcomeGreeting = persona opening_line e.g. "Hello?"); later (2 / not-now words) -> `postpone_call` (status scheduled, now+2h, pushed inside store hours via next_slot if needed, attempts -1, outcome postponed) + Say goodbye; none (silence, voicemail greeting captured as SpeechResult, other digits) -> record_outcome "no_response" ("Went to voicemail or wasn't ready") + Say "No problem, we'll try again another time. This was your practice call from I'm On Social." -> Hangup. No grade, no scorecard text, no recording kept (save_recording skips scheduled/unreachable/canceled). Session fields gate_passed_at, gate_via.
- Inbound nudge: relay websocket uses asyncio.wait_for; if the rep says nothing for INBOUND_NUDGE_S=8 s after setup on an inbound call, `relay_nudge` speaks the opening line and records it (customer_turn's inbound branch now only fires when turns==[] so the opening never repeats). Applies to inbound practice calls too.
- Hard cap: Twilio `time_limit=CALL_TIME_LIMIT_S` (18 min) on shop AND practice calls.
- Direction now honored: create_shop_call uses the challenge's direction (default inbound); grade_session/evaluation direction from the session; ChallengeBody.direction + editor chips "Inbound: they call the store" / "Outbound: rep calls them" (testids challenge-direction-inbound/-outbound), detail sheet shows it. shop_greeting ("Hi, is this Bud?") removed.
- Quick shops: record_outcome never reschedules when client.demo (unreachable right away, label without "(n tries)"); OUTCOME_LABEL gained no_response + postponed. Scorecard SMS: "that practice call just now was from I'm On Social...". Customer prompt: rep knows it's practice but AI stays in character.

- TEST: backend/tests/mystery_shop_e2e.py rewritten for the gate (announce -> voicemail speech = no grade + rescheduled -> press 2 = postponed 120 min attempts back -> "ready" = ring + relay -> silent rep nudge 7.6 s -> full graded call; quick shop voicemail -> unreachable no retry; outbound challenge announcement + "Hello?" greeting). ALL OK on preview.

## Industry Agnostic Mystery Shops, Phase 1 (June 2026) - DONE, tested (backend tests/mystery_shop_e2e.py ALL OK; real-estate client curl pass; testing_agent iteration_319 frontend 100%, 0 issues)
- USER: "what do we need to change to make this industry agnostic? I can sell this to any industry" -> "start phase one now". Automotive (sales/service/parts/rental) was hardcoded in prompts, plans, reports, pickers and public pages.
- ARCHITECTURE: `backend/services/industries.py` = INDUSTRY PACKS (automotive, real_estate, home_services, medical_dental, insurance, fitness, property_management). Each pack: label, nouns (business/place/customer/trainer), offering {label, plural, hint, field, field_help} (replaces "vehicle"), departments [{key (globally unique, e.g. re_buyer, hs_repair), label, call, prefix, rep, brief, curveballs, defaults, template (automotive -> scorecards.py templates) | scorecard {name, criteria}}]. Helpers: get/key_of/departments/dept_keys/all_dept_keys/dept/industry_of_dept/dept_label/dept_options (key,label,call,rep)/label_map/for_api/fill_offering ({vehicle}/{offering}/{store} placeholders).
- DATA: shop_clients.industry (default automotive), plan.per_month {dept_key: n} (legacy sales_per_month/service_per_month still read + echoed for old clients), shop_clients.vehicles doubles as "offerings" (serialize returns both). scripts (pool mystery_shop) carry industry (backfilled automotive). roleplay_sessions carry industry. Proposals terms.per_month.
- BACKEND: serialize_client returns industry, industry_label, departments, offering, customer_noun; build_report.by_department[dept] has label and iterates the client's departments + any stray call departments; plan_month/plan_active_clients iterate dept keys (fixed the last `made["sales"]+made["service"]` KeyError); template_card(dept) grades from the pack scorecard (automotive keeps scorecards.py templates); `GET /api/shop-clients/industries` (+ per-dept challenge counts); `POST /api/shop-clients/challenges/seed {industry, department?}` Jessi writes 2 starters per empty department (~30-60 s each, frontend seeds one department per request with 180 s timeout); challenge_pool filters by department or industry; demo_shop accepts industry; public proposal returns per_month/departments/offering/business_noun; kickoff returns departments (with rep) + offering; public_score returns department_label/customer_noun; report.client carries industry/customer_noun/business_noun. Announcement + customer prompt + generator already used pack nouns.
- FRONTEND (`components/mystery-shops/shared.tsx`): industries load once from the API (`loadIndustries`, `industries()`, `industryOf`, `deptsFor`, `deptsOfClient(client)`, `deptLabel(key, depts?)`, `perMonthText(per, joiner, depts?)`, `stripTitlePrefix`). `DEPTS` constant is now only the automotive fallback. Every picker/label reads from the client's `departments`: PeopleTab (groups + chips + title placeholder from dept.rep), CallsTab (plan button + toast + "caller/shopper" noun + transcript label), BillingTab (per-department proposal fields `proposal-per-{dept}`), ChallengesTab/ChallengeGroups (`departments` prop, `renderEmpty`), ChallengeEditorSheet + GeneratorSheet (industry chips `challenge-industry-*` / `generator-industry-*` when not inside a client; dept chips follow), DemoShopSheet (industry chips `demo-industry-*`, dept chips, brokerage/company wording, offering label, `demo-no-challenges` + disabled call when the pool is empty), ReportView (iterates by_department with labels), list card per-dept progress `shop-client-progress-{id}` + industry in subtitle, detail header "Real estate · 4 buyer leads + 2 seller leads / mo", library screen industry chip row `library-industry-*` + purple `library-seed` / `library-seed-{dept}` Jessi-writes-the-starters actions. Public pages: proposal tiles per department, kickoff offerings card/title/placeholder + KickoffPeople grouped by the account's departments ("Move to X" cycles), hours summary "Our caller calls...", shop report intro + dept cards, shop score meta.
- PHASE 2 (not started): per-industry scorecard templates in the main Scorecards feature (scorecards.py DEPARTMENTS still automotive), Courses department chips (sales|service|parts|rental|mixed), PDF/SMS wording audit, CATEGORY_BY_DEPT, industry-specific Quick-shop defaults. Practice scripts (STARTER_SCRIPTS) remain automotive.
- SHIP: backend Deploy + `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Industry packs: sell Mystery Shops to any vertical"`.

## Quick Shops: no hours, no schedule (June 2026) - DONE, e2e ALL OK
- USER: "With the demo practice calls let's eliminate the quiet hours or schedule all together so it works 100% of the time if I put one in."
- FIX (services/mystery_shops.py): `ALWAYS_OPEN`; `_hours()`/`in_hours()` short-circuit for `demo` clients (stored hours ignored), `next_slot()` for demo = exactly `after + gap` (no random spread across the day, which previously could push a retry ~20 h out); `run_due_calls` never hours-gates demo calls; `rename_legacy_quick_bucket` forces hours=ALWAYS_OPEN + active=True on the production bucket at first load; `dial_now()` claims the row (scheduled -> dialing) before placing so the 2-min scheduler can never double-dial an immediate shop (used by `demo_shop` and `POST /{cid}/calls/shop-now`); `postpone_call` for Quick shops parks the shop as unreachable/"Asked us to call back later" (admin taps Try again) instead of booking a 2-h callback. Announcement (services/scripts.shop_announcement) for demo sessions ends "press 2 and we'll try another time"; gate reply matches. Quick shop sheet copy explains it. Test added to mystery_shop_e2e.py.
- SHIP: backend Deploy + `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Quick shops dial instantly, any hour"`.
- FOLLOW-UP (same day): user still saw "queued for Monday morning" IN PRODUCTION (fix above not deployed yet; today was a Sunday). Root cause for the other human-fired paths: `record_outcome` retried Shop now / course Call now / Try again shops via `next_slot` inside business hours, and `postpone_call` booked press-2 callbacks into hours too. FIX: `hand_fired = demo or call.manual` -> never auto-retry (parks as unreachable, no "(n tries)" suffix, no course 24-h re-queue), press 2 parks as postponed, announcement/gate say "try another time" for manual sessions; course `schedule_next_shop(immediate=True)` creates the call for now with manual=True; retry + course shop-now use `dial_now()`. Only automatic monthly/course shops keep the inside-hours retry. e2e updated (automatic sim uses manual=False; new manual-in-real-account block). ALL OK.
- PRODUCTION PHONES NOT RINGING (diagnosed from Twilio logs, not code): production dials from toll-free (844) 921-6102; callee carriers return SIP 603 Decline (shown as "Line was busy") or send straight to voicemail; STIR attestation B. Told the user to pick a local number in the Shop caller number card ((435) 220-3414 / (801) 797-3298 / (385) 444-3045 / (385) 533-5263). OPEN OFFER (not built): label SIP 603/607/608 as "Their carrier declined the call (spam filter). Switch to a local caller number" instead of "Line was busy"; warn on toll-free numbers in the picker; Trust Hub SHAKEN/STIR + Voice Integrity enrollment (Number Health backlog).

## Sold Units = Purchase Records (June 2026) - DONE, tested (backend tests/sales_units_test.py ALL OK; web smoke: Bridger shows Jul 13 Road Glide in July AND Sep 1 Supra in September, contact page dates match)
- USER (frustrated): Bridger Ward bought a Road Glide in July and a Supra in September; the July unit vanished from Sold Units, Purchase History lost/duplicated rows, and the same sale read Aug 31 on the list vs Sep 1 on the contact. "A customer can purchase more than one thing... think through the logic and fix it."
- ROOT CAUSES: (1) Sold Units/monthly summary/home tiles counted CONTACTS by the single `date_sold` field, so a second sale moved the customer to the new month. (2) Two writers fought over purchase_history: the wizard's `POST /purchases` row + `record_sale`'s "repeat" archive row `{date, vehicle, notes:'Previous purchase', is_repeat}` (no id/title), both `$inc sold_count`. (3) Dates travelled as timestamps (toISOString / UTC midnight) so Utah midnight crossed the day line; PurchaseHistorySection saved `toISOString().split('T')[0]` (next day after 6 PM MDT).
- MODEL (`backend/services/sales.py`, rewritten): `purchase_history[]` = the sold log for the customer, one record per purchase `{id, title, category, date 'YYYY-MM-DD'|None, notes, created_at, source}`. `date_sold` (naive midnight of the latest dated record), `vehicle` (latest title), `sold_count` (len) are DERIVED by `save_entries()`. `record_sale(cid, day, title, category, only_if_unsold, source)`: new calendar day (and no same-title record within 7 days) = NEW record, earlier ones untouched; same day or same title within 7 days = correction of that record (wizard re-runs, backdating, old two-call flow date-only then title). `add_purchase/update_purchase/delete_purchase` re-derive. `sync_from_contact_fields` = contact-form Date Sold edits the latest record. `units_pipeline/count_units/units_by_month/list_units` = aggregation that `$unwind`s records (legacy date_sold-only contacts count as one unit). `repair_all()` = one-time additive migration (dedupe blank/duplicate archive rows, write a record for a sale that only lived in date_sold/vehicle, drop prev_vehicle, re-derive) run at startup under `migrations._id = sales_units_v2_2026_09` (preview: scanned 6, fixed 6).
- ENDPOINTS: `PATCH /contacts/{u}/{c}/date-sold {date, title?, category?}` records the sale in ONE call (returns sale: first|repeat|same, sold_count, date_sold 'YYYY-MM-DD'); `GET/POST/PUT/DELETE /contacts/{u}/{c}/purchases` go through the service; `PUT /contacts/{u}/{c}` normalizes date_sold to the calendar day, never blanks/forks it, and syncs the latest record; contact create with date_sold writes the first record; sold_workflow tag path uses `record_sale(only_if_unsold)`; `PATCH /vehicle` no longer stashes prev_vehicle. server.py `sold-contacts` (one row per unit: `unit_id`, `vehicle`=record title, `date_sold`='YYYY-MM-DD'), `sold-monthly-summary`, `sold-performance`, `team/{u}/performance` all count units.
- FRONTEND: `sales-list.tsx` renders `fmtDay('YYYY-MM-DD')` from parts (no Date parsing), key `${_id}-${unit_id}`, testid `sales-unit-date-{unit_id}`; `PurchaseHistorySection` saves local `toYmd`, parses `fromYmd`, shows 'Purchase' for untitled rows, `onChanged` -> DetailsTab `reloadContact`; `contact/[id].tsx handleSave` sends birthday/anniversary/date_sold/custom_dates as local 'YYYY-MM-DD'; `sold-quick.tsx` + `contact/sold-wizard.tsx` send `{date, title, category}` to date-sold in one call (dropped the separate purchases POST and the broken PUT {vehicle}).
- Preview verification contact: "Bridger Demo-Units" 6aa7425eefb7a2e43806c16e (Road Glide 2026-07-13, Supra 2026-09-01) under forest@imosapp.com.
- SHIP: backend Deploy (migration runs itself once) + `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Sold Units count every purchase; calendar-safe dates"`.
- PURCHASE DETAIL ROWS (same session, DONE, web smoke ok): Sold Units rows show a category icon + the unit's title (`sales-unit-vehicle-{unit_id}`) and a purple "Nx BUYER" chip whenever the customer has more than one record; tapping a row pushes `/contact/{id}?purchase={unit_id}`; `contact/[id].tsx` reads `purchase`, lands on Details, and `PurchaseHistorySection` (prop `focusPurchaseId`) outlines that record in gold (`purchase-row-{id}`, style `focusedRow`) and opens its Edit Purchase sheet once (legacy-<contactId> ids fall back to the newest record). Header copy: "one row per unit · tap a unit to open that purchase".

## Mystery Shops iOS cleanup: dead attach link, freezes, keyboard (June 2026) - DONE (web smoke ok; iOS keyboard/modal behaviour needs device verification after eas update)
- USER: "attach a PDF or a file, that link is dead"; "app freezes inside the mystery shop window, have to close the app"; "keyboard covers the field so I can't see what I'm texting".
- ROOT CAUSE (1+2): `ChallengeEditorSheet` rendered `ImportScriptSheet` (a second RN `Modal`) as a sibling of its own `Sheet` Modal. iOS presents one modal at a time: the second never appeared ("dead link") and could leave an invisible touch-eating modal behind (freeze). Same hazard in the generator -> editor and detail -> editor hand-offs and Quick shop / new client -> navigate, which opened/pushed in the same tick the first modal closed.
- FIX: `components/scripts/ImportScriptSheet.tsx` now exports `ImportScriptPanel` (plain content) + `ImportScriptSheet` (Modal wrapper for full screens like scripts/editor). The challenge editor renders the panel INLINE inside its sheet (`challenge-import` toggles it, `script-import-close` collapses). `shared.afterModal(fn)` (450 ms on iOS) wraps every modal-to-modal / modal-to-navigate hand-off (ChallengesTab, library, index onSaved/onStarted).
- REAL FILE ATTACH: new `POST /api/scripts/import-file` (multipart; pypdf for PDF, python-docx for .docx, utf-8 for text; 8 MB cap; scanned-PDF/no-text -> 422 with a paste hint) feeding the same `import_script_text` shaper; panel button `script-import-file` uses expo-document-picker (web: blob; native: uri). `import_script_text` now tolerates the model returning `body` as a list (was crashing with 'list has no attribute strip').
- KEYBOARD: `Sheet` provides `SheetScrollCtx.ensureVisible(node)`; `Field` (and the import panel via `useSheetScroll`) calls it on focus; it measures the input's page Y against keyboard top (from `keyboardDidShow`) minus the footer height and scrolls the sheet's ScrollView so the field sits above the keyboard (re-runs 80/400 ms and on keyboardDidShow). ScrollView also has `keyboardDismissMode="interactive"`.
- SHIP: backend Deploy (pypdf/python-docx added to requirements.txt) + `cd frontend && eas update --branch production --message "Mystery Shops: attach PDF/Word scripts, no more frozen sheets, keyboard-safe fields"`.
- FOLLOW-UP: user (production) made a non-automotive challenge and "everything on the scorecard said dealership". Audit: generator (`generate_challenges`), grading (`template_card` pack scorecards, `grade_with_ai` coach) and challenge pools are per-industry (verified: insurance quote challenge, zero dealership/vehicle words). The leak was the paste/attach import: `import_script_text` was hard-wired "You are Jessi, a dealership sales trainer" with {vehicle}/{store} merge fields. FIX: `import_script_text(text, industry, department)` builds the prompt from the pack (trainer, business, customer, offering, dept brief; {offering} merge field, automotive keeps {vehicle}); `POST /scripts/import` body + `POST /scripts/import-file` query accept industry/department; `ImportScriptPanel` sends them; ChallengeEditorSheet passes the picked industry/department. "the dealership" fallback store_name in practice sessions -> "the store". Verified: insurance quote script import -> agency wording, {store}/{offering}, no dealership.
